## 社团介绍

A Cappella Club is a student-led vocal ensemble where members create music entirely through the human voice, without instrumental accompaniment. The club welcomes students with different levels of singing experience and provides a supportive environment for members to develop their vocal and performance skills. During weekly sessions, members will learn fundamental a cappella techniques, including vocal warm-ups, breathing, resonance, harmony, blending, rhythm, and ensemble listening. Members will be assigned suitable voice parts based on their vocal ranges and will work together to rehearse arrangements for school performances such as SGT and other events throughout the year. Beyond preparing for performances, the club aims to build a close and collaborative musical community. Through rehearsals, section practices, and performances, members will learn not only how to become more confident singers, but also how to listen, communicate, and contribute effectively as part of an ensemble.

## 负责人

## 社团介绍

SCIE Buddhist Wisdom Club introduces classical Buddhist ideas in a simple and open way. Members study compassion, karma, anger, meditation, and emptiness through selected texts and school-life examples. Teachers and students can ask questions and join respectful discussions. People of all beliefs are welcome; no religious commitment is required.

# AI Ethics & Governance Roundtable

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5210/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 3 |
| Name | AI Ethics & Governance Roundtable |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 23291 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Social sciences |
| Room | A206 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Thursday LunchTime |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

AI Ethics & Governance Roundtable is a source-based discussion and writing ECA for students interested in how artificial intelligence should be developed, regulated and used. Each meeting examines a concrete policy question through primary materials such as legislation, regulatory guidance, institutional frameworks, research papers and real cases. Topics may include risk-based AI regulation, transparency and labelling of AI-generated content, copyright and training data, algorithmic bias, biometric surveillance, education, open-source models, frontier-model safety and responsibility for automated decisions. Members will distinguish facts, legal requirements, ethical principles and stakeholder interests before joining a moderated roundtable. They will then write short position memos that state a claim, address counterarguments and propose an actionable policy. No prior legal or technical knowledge is required. The aim is not to reach one approved opinion, but to develop evidence-based judgment, careful disagreement and responsible public reasoning. By the end of the semester, members will produce a reviewed collection of position papers and present selected arguments in a public AI governance forum.

## 负责人

- Alvin — Student s23291.li@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Thursday
Time: LunchTime
Classroom: A206

# Aerodynamicists

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5112/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 3 |
| Name | Aerodynamicists |
| Leader | ELH |
| Leadership Team | 25408 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Technology and engineering |
| Room | A419 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

The Aerodynamics Club is for students interested in understanding how air flows around objects and how these principles are applied in engineering. This semester, the club will introduce key topics such as fluid properties, pressure, viscosity, boundary layers, lift, drag, flow separation, and airfoil design. Sessions will combine short explanations with demonstrations, discussions, and practical activities so that members can connect physical theory with real aerodynamic applications. As the semester progresses, students will also be introduced to computational fluid dynamics (CFD), learning how computer simulations can be used to visualise and analyse airflow. Depending on members’ experience and interest, we will explore software such as OpenFOAM and PyFR and conduct simple aerodynamic modelling projects. No previous knowledge of aerodynamics or coding is required. The club is designed to be accessible to beginners while also offering opportunities for more experienced students to explore advanced topics. Our goal is to develop members’ understanding of physics, engineering, numerical modelling, and real-world aerodynamic design.

## 负责人

- Elva — Teacher elva.he@scie.com.cn
- Cindy — Student s25408.xin@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Monday
Time: Period 11 (First period after school)
Classroom: A419

# Aerodynamics & Simulation Club

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5134/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 4 |
| Name | Aerodynamics & Simulation Club |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 24460 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Technology and engineering |
| Room | A406 |
| Students | 5 |
| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Ever wondered why a Formula 1 car sticks to the ground, how aircraft wings generate lift, or how engineers test a design before it is ever built? In this ECA, you will turn your own ideas into 3D models using Rhino, then bring them into STAR-CCM+ to see how air, fluids, heat and other physical forces interact with your designs. Instead of simply learning software, you will work like an engineer: design, simulate, analyze, improve, and test again. Projects may include aerodynamic cars, aircraft wings, drones, turbines, cooling systems, or even designs of your own choice. No previous experience with 3D modelling or simulation is required—we will start from the basics and gradually move toward more advanced projects. If you enjoy engineering, physics, design, cars, aviation, or simply want to see whether your ideas actually work in the real world, this ECA gives you a chance to explore engineering beyond the classroom.

## 负责人

- Rayman — Student s24460.guo@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Tuesday
Time: Period 11 (First period after school)
Classroom: A406

## 社团介绍

The Animal Science and Welfare Club is a student-led club exploring animal biology, behaviour, health, and welfare. Through discussions, presentations, case studies, interactive activities, and research, members will learn about how animals function, behave, and interact with humans. The club also examines real-world issues such as responsible animal care, conservation, animal ethics, and the challenges faced by animals in different environments. Our aim is to combine scientific knowledge with empathy and encourage students to develop a deeper understanding of animals and our responsibilities towards them.

## 社团介绍

Starting and evolving from SCIE RC Racing Club last year, Apex Racing is a brand new and fully developed RC racing team, that not only focus on engineering and racing, but also teamwork, collaboration skills, and abilities to adapt to new tasks. We need more than tech geeks, finance and public media department needs much more members to join us and fight together. In the ECA we are going to form a real racing team, with its typical structure. Members will have hand-on experience of dealing with a racing season, from the start with preparations, to the end winning the grand prize. During practice, everyone will have the opportunity to drive the 1/10 Touring Car to perform tasks, achieve standard lap time, and learn from the ECA leader about all technical knowledge related to RC racing cars. These knowledges can also be used on real cars. Furthermore, all spendings and costs during the season will rely on sponsors, and thus members do not need to pay for any additional material costs. In collaboration with other ECAs, we will be studying for all ranges of information. Feel free to join and enjoy a wonderful journey with professional Remote controlled cars.

# AnthroPulse

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5211/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| Category | SOCIAL Interests and hobbies |
| Room | A219 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

The AnthnoPulse Lab is an anthropology-focused club dedicated to uncovering the hidden meanings in everyday life. We treat our school and city as living field sites, investigating how objects, spaces, and routines reflect human behavior and social change. Unlike traditional academic clubs, we don’t just read about culture—we go out to observe it. Through systematic fieldwork, interviews, and creative documentation, we explore questions like: How do students personalize their dorm spaces? How do different generations use the same public park? What do market transactions reveal about community values? Each semester, members collaborate on a micro-ethnography project, producing field notes, interview excerpts, and visual responses. The AnthnoPulse club is a space for curious minds to develop observational rigor, cultural empathy, and a nuanced understanding of the diverse communities we inhabit.

## 负责人

- Lily — Student s24236.zhou@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Thursday
Time: Period 11 (First period after school)
Classroom: A219

## 社团介绍

Formerly Introduction to Architecture, we broaden the content and aim to dismantle the traditional barriers between STEM and the Arts. Inspired by the interdisciplinary mindset championed by top universities like Harvard and Cambridge, we create a platform where members explore the connection between diverse fields like art, science, history, and technology. We hold lectures, discussions and hands-on activities, displaying our members’ work through an exhibition. Our curriculum includes learning foundational modelling skills (Blender, Rhino) and engaging in themed interdisciplinary lectures and experiments. ## 社团介绍

Starting and evolving from SCIE RC Racing Club last year, Apex Racing is a brand new and fully developed RC racing team, that not only focus on engineering and racing, but also teamwork, collaboration skills, and abilities to adapt to new tasks. We need more than tech geeks, finance and public media department needs much more members to join us and fight together. In the ECA we are going to form a real racing team, with its typical structure. Members will have hand-on experience of dealing with a racing season, from the start with preparations, to the end winning the grand prize. During practice, everyone will have the opportunity to drive the 1/10 Touring Car to perform tasks, achieve standard lap time, and learn from the ECA leader about all technical knowledge related to RC racing cars. These knowledges can also be used on real cars. Furthermore, all spendings and costs during the season will rely on sponsors, and thus members do not need to pay for any additional material costs. In collaboration with other ECAs, we will be studying for all ranges of information. Feel free to join and enjoy a wonderful journey with professional Remote controlled cars.

## 社团介绍

BioChem Fever is a student-led club designed to ignite curiosity and creativity in the fields of biology and chemistry. Our activities combine hands-on experiments, scientific discussions, and problem-based projects to help members deepen their understanding of the natural sciences beyond the classroom. The club will cover molecular biology, biochemistry, and applications in biotechnology, giving students the opportunity to explore fascinating topics such as enzyme activity, synthetic biology, and environmental applications of biochemistry. By integrating practical experiments with theory, members will gain confidence in experimental design, data analysis, and scientific communication. BioChem Fever aims to be both educational and enjoyable, cultivating teamwork and inspiring future scientists.

# Bureau of Unexplained Affairs

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5114/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| --- | --- |
| NO. | 15 |
| Name | Bureau of Unexplained Affairs |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 26804 |
| Category | SOCIAL Interests and hobbies |
| Room | A201 |
| Students | 3 |
| Period | Monday Period13 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

This ECA is a civil‑paranormal‑phenomenon research society. We explore reported anomalous cases, including UFO sightings, cryptids such as the Loch Ness Monster, and historical supernatural folklore. Rather than confirming whether paranormal events are real, our core goal is to analyse these accounts from historical, psychological and sociological perspectives. We will study classic materials such as 《Soulstealers: The Chinese Sorcery Scare of 1768》, comparing mass panics in history with modern‑day viral supernatural rumours. Members practise critical‑thinking, source‑checking and group discussion. We evaluate eyewitness reports, media narratives and social‑psychological causes behind collective belief.

## 负责人

- Nora — Student s26804.pan@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Monday
Time: Period 13 (Random time after school)
Classroom: A201

## 社团介绍

The Biobío club is a student-led extracurricular activity dedicated to exploring biology through hands-on activities, field observation, and interdisciplinary projects. Rather than focusing on lectures or examination preparation, the club aims to make biology engaging and relevant to everyday life. Activities will include campus biodiversity surveys, species identification, and investigations into the biology behind familiar experiences such as plant pigments, fragrances, food, and nutrition. By connecting ecology, environmental science, molecular biology, and nutrition, members will explore the diversity of biological sciences while developing their own interests. The club welcomes students with different levels of biological knowledge and encourages curiosity, collaboration, and independent exploration.

Rather than confirming whether paranormal events are real, our core goal is to analyse these accounts from historical, psychological and sociological perspectives. We will study classic materials such as 《Soulstealers: The Chinese Sorcery Scare of 1768》, comparing mass panics in history with modern‑day viral supernatural rumours. Members practise critical‑thinking, source‑checking and group discussion. We evaluate eyewitness reports, media narratives and social‑psychological causes behind collective belief.

## 负责人

- Nora — Student s26804.pan@stu.scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Monday
Time: Period 13 (Random time after school)
Classroom: A201

# Cosmology Horizon

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5169/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 29 |
| Name | Cosmology Horizon |
| Leader | SEM |
| Leadership Team | 24095 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Natural sciences |
| Room | B523 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Our ECA is an astronomy outreach club that aims to make astronomy accessible, engaging, and relevant to students with different levels of scientific background. Through regular student-led sessions, we introduce topics ranging from neutron stars, stellar evolution, and auroras to asteroid defense, Mars exploration, and the future of human spaceflight. Rather than focusing on highly technical theory, our sessions emphasize clear explanations, visual materials, discussion, and curiosity-driven exploration. A distinctive part of our ECA is our astronomy photography program. We have access to remotely operated telescopes located at astronomical observatories in Ningxia and other regions of China. Members can apply for observing time and use these telescopes to capture astronomical objects under the guidance of the current and former club presidents. This gives students an opportunity to move beyond learning about astronomy and actually observe and photograph the universe themselves. We also organize an annual visit to the Shenzhen Astronomical Observatory, where members can experience professional astronomical facilities and learn about the daily work of astronomers.

- Sean — Teacher sean.mather@scie.com.cn
- Aesop — Student s24095.zhu@stu.scie.com.cn

## 社团介绍

DSM is a club that intends to focus on the behavioural and developmental aspects of psychology. We aim to expand students' knowledge of various types of psychological experiments by incorporating literature and media into the lessons. The club seeks to explore the psychological theories and disorders reflected in literary and media characters, and explain the experiments done by past psychologists involved with the disorder (e.g Freud with Harry Potter dreams, or tie Pavlov to 1984 by George Orwell). At the end of every session, the club leader and members are encouraged to come up with a short story with characters sharing the same mental health problem. By the end of the semester, the club will compile the stories into a student-produced psychology magazine/journal, joining creative storytelling with rigorous academic insights.

## 社团介绍

Our ECA focuses on neurodiversity and cognitive accessibility in school environments. We will introduce the concept of "cognitive variations" that result from genetic differences, the environments in which people grow up, and differences in how people think, and then explore how these variations may influence students’ experiences in school. Then, we will explain and examine cognitive barriers, such as ADHD, dyslexia, and autism, that can arise from everyday learning materials, classroom environments, and communication methods. Each session will focus on a different aspect of neurodiversity or cognitive accessibility, such as attention, memory, reading, sensory processing, or executive function. The most important component of CogniAble ECA is to investigate practical ways to reduce them. The ECA aims to connect concepts from cognitive science with practical changes in the school environment, with a focus on making information and everyday school experiences more cognitively accessible.

## 社团介绍

Cognitive Aquarium is an experiment-based cognitive science and behavioural science club that requires no prerequisite in psychology background. We will give an intro about what is cognitive science and specify its comprising blocks and components. Participants will ideally gain skills on reading, writing, interpreting and summarising research studies throughout hands-on experiment process. Each typical session will be a deep dive into a specific cognitive science concepts—e.g. memory, decision-making, attention, perception, etc— and usually includes 1. A short lecture/explanation 2. An activity (such as a game, a social interaction game, or other interactive tasks) 3. Replication of an classical or fun experiment (with participants trying to summarise the results) 4. A post-class assignment to read a relevant paper, discuss interesting ideas or "fun facts" gleaned from it, and log them in a shared document.

## 社团介绍

Earth Three-body Organization was founded in 2023. Inspired by the sci-fi "The Three-body Problem," this community was founded to gather enthusiasts of the book, given its widespread influence around the globe. After reforms, we held weekly seminars discussing the literary, scientific and humanitarian aspects of the novel. Everyone will have a nametag, and will be invited by the host to share their thoughts in the seminars. Such activity format was inspired by US liberal arts based education, valuing interdisciplinary, freedom of expression and belonging. Apart from weekly activities, ETO also held projects. In 2024, we attempted to invite the book's author, Mr. Cixin Liu, to give speech at SCIE, but received a kind rejection. Last year, we successfully invited the department head of Astronomy of Sun Yat-sen Univeristy, Prof. Jun Cui, to speak at SCIE, making tangible community impact. Next year, we will be focusing on publishing and creating an archive to the content of our discussion

This ECA combines introductory social‑psychological knowledge with light‑hearted recreational activities to promote accessible mental‑well‑being awareness among students. We deliver beginner‑friendly psychology tips covering emotion regulation, stress management and self‑acceptance, avoiding overly complex academic jargon. Most sessions prioritize entertaining interactive segments, including casual group discussions, mini games and scenario‑sharing, instead of rigid lectures. Members will learn practical, daily‑usable mental‑health skills to recognize personal emotional signals and build healthier mindsets. This activity creates a safe, relaxed peer space, where participants can reduce psychological pressure, understand simple social‑psychological logic, and support one another’s mental wellness in an enjoyable atmosphere.

## 社团介绍

EduSpark is a student-led education and social impact ECA that aims to make learning more engaging, accessible, and connected to the wider world. Members explore real-world topics such as culture, history, economics, technology, mathematics, sustainability, and global inequality, and transform what they learn into interactive educational activities. Rather than simply volunteering as tutors, members will work collaboratively to design discussion-based lessons, educational games, workshops, and outreach projects for younger students and communities with fewer opportunities to access diverse perspectives. The ECA is built around four principles: curiosity, critical thinking, global perspective, and student empowerment. Members will develop their own ideas, test activities, receive feedback, and improve them through reflection. Our work will include both on-campus educational activities and community outreach, including the EduSpark Shanxi teaching project. Through these experiences, we hope to create a sustainable cycle: students learn, students design, students teach, and students create positive social impact. Ultimately, EduSpark aims to help both our members and the students we reach see a bigger world, think more deeply, and believe that they can make a difference.

## 社团介绍

Our ECA is an astronomy outreach club that aims to make astronomy accessible, engaging, and relevant to students with different levels of scientific background. Through regular student-led sessions, we introduce topics ranging from neutron stars, stellar evolution, and auroras to asteroid defense, Mars exploration, and the future of human spaceflight. Rather than focusing on highly technical theory, our sessions emphasize clear explanations, visual materials, discussion, and curiosity-driven exploration. A distinctive part of our ECA is our astronomy photography program. We have access to remotely operated telescopes located at astronomical observatories in Ningxia and other regions of China. Members can apply for observing time and use these telescopes to capture astronomical objects under the guidance of the current and former club presidents. This gives students an opportunity to move beyond learning about astronomy and actually observe and photograph the universe themselves. We also organize an annual visit to the Shenzhen Astronomical Observatory, where members can experience professional astronomical facilities and learn about the daily work of astronomers.

## 社团介绍

The Electromagnetics & Optics Society is a theory‑oriented club focused on the fundamental principles of electricity, magnetism, and light. Our sessions cover topics such as Ohm’s law, electric fields, potential energy, Lorentz force, magnetic flux, electromagnetic induction, Lenz’s law, motional and induced EMF, photoelectric effect, double‑slit interference, grating diffraction, and half‑wave loss. We meet weekly for guided discussions and problem‑solving sessions. The emphasis is on building a clear conceptual understanding and working through challenging questions together. Simple demonstrations may be included occasionally to help visualize abstract ideas, but the main focus remains on theoretical reasoning and group discussion. Members are encouraged to ask questions and share their thinking. All students with an interest in physics are welcome, regardless of prior background.

## 社团介绍

DECODE THE WORLD is an interdisciplinary and project-based ECA that explores how mathematics, data, computer science, and scientific thinking can be used to understand and solve real-world problems. Each year we decode a different domain by breaking complex problems into measurable components to find patterns in data and building computational solutions. This year's theme is DECODE SOUND, exploring the science and technology behind sound through signal processing, Fourier analysis, spectrograms, algorithms, and AI. At the end of every session there is a short technical lesson with a practical experiment or mini-build, allowing members to immediately apply what they learn. No prior coding (we will be doing vibe coding) or music experience is required. The goal to learn how to apply knowledge from different disciplines to problems beyond the classroom

## 社团介绍

The Entrepreneurship Club aims to introduce students to both the theoretical and practical sides of entrepreneurship. Inspired by my experience at LaunchX, where I studied entrepreneurial concepts and developed a startup with my team, I wanted to create a space at school for students who are interested in business but may not yet have opportunities to explore entrepreneurship in practice. Through interactive workshops, case discussions, and group projects, members learn how to identify market opportunities, conduct market research, develop value propositions and MVPs, understand basic business models and finance, and pitch ideas effectively. Rather than focusing only on lectures, the club encourages students to apply these concepts to real-world problems. For example, members have discussed developing a platform that connects food-delivery services while providing calorie information, personalized dietary planning, and restaurant recommendations for users with fitness goals. Ultimately, the club hopes to help students become more observant, creative, and analytical problem-solvers while gaining practical experience in turning an initial idea into a viable business concept.

We organize intramural games and pick-up games, which allow members to play in a more casual setting. Additionally, the members of the dodgeball club will be able to participate in the once-in-a-year dodgeball house event, providing our members with the opportunity to showcase their abilities and test their mettle against other players. Overall, our goal is to provide a fun and inclusive environment for students to enjoy the game of dodgeball, while also helping them to improve their physical fitness and teamwork abilities.

## 社团介绍

DSM is a club that intends to focus on the behavioural and developmental aspects of psychology. We aim to expand students' knowledge of various types of psychological experiments by incorporating literature and media into the lessons. The club seeks to explore the psychological theories and disorders reflected in literary and media characters, and explain the experiments done by past psychologists involved with the disorder (e.g Freud with Harry Potter dreams, or tie Pavlov to 1984 by George Orwell). At the end of every session, the club leader and members are encouraged to come up with a short story with characters sharing the same mental health problem. By the end of the semester, the club will compile the stories into a student-produced psychology magazine/journal, joining creative storytelling with rigorous academic insights.

# Flawesome Museum Club

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5097/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| Leader |  |

| Category | SOCIAL Volunteers and charitable projects |
| Room | B531 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Friday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

This ECA uses art and storytelling to explore the imperfections, challenges, and unexpected moments that shape people’s lives. This year, its main focus will be elderly care and the experiences of older adults, using creative expression to encourage students to look beyond stereotypes of aging and understand the people behind them. Students will visit elderly-care communities and related facilities, where they will observe daily life, conduct interviews, and explore topics such as aging, memories, family relationships, loneliness, independence, dignity, and the changing relationship between generations. They will work together to create a documentary that records these experiences and gives elderly participants an opportunity to share their own stories and perspectives. Alongside documentary filmmaking, students will transform what they learn into different forms of art, including photography, painting, writing, illustration, and installation. These works will explore themes such as memory, aging, vulnerability, resilience, and human connection. The creative process will encourage students to reflect on their own assumptions about aging while developing empathy, communication, and storytelling skills. The ECA will culminate in an exhibition combining student artwork, documentary footage, photographs, and other materials collected throughout the project. By bringing these stories to a wider audience, the exhibition will encourage conversations about elderly care and intergenerational understanding, ultimately turning individual stories and everyday experiences into meaningful narratives of connection.

## 社团介绍

This is a geology club where students can explore the forces and processes that shape the Earth. Each secession begins with an intriguing earth mystery. Why do earthquakes and volcanoes occur in plate boundaries? How can seismic waves reveal Earth’s interior? Why are some mountains red? How do glaciers create fjords, and how can ocean temperatures affect weather around the world? We will first go through the basic knowledge of geology such as rock cycle and sedimentary processes. Then we will talk about interesting geographical phenomena and landscapes. Some topics and activities will be selected through member voting, allowing the club to reflect students’ interests. We will use maps, photographs, rock samples, models, real data and group challenges to examine evidence and develop explanations.

The French Film ECA is a space for students to watch and discuss a wide range of French-language films together. Through films from different genres, periods, and French-speaking regions, members will explore French culture, society, history, and cinematic aesthetics. After each film, members will be encouraged to share their personal views and interpretations in a friendly and open discussion. We believe that listening to different opinions can help us notice details and perspectives that we may have missed on our own. Through these discussions, we hope to develop a deeper appreciation of cinema and a more thoughtful way of viewing the world. The ECA welcomes everyone, regardless of their level of French or previous knowledge of film. English subtitles will be provided when necessary. Our main aim is to enjoy French-language cinema together while learning from the films and from one another.

## 社团介绍

Earth Three-body Organization was founded in 2023. Inspired by the sci-fi "The Three-body Problem," this community was founded to gather enthusiasts of the book, given its widespread influence around the globe. After reforms, we held weekly seminars discussing the literary, scientific and humanitarian aspects of the novel. Everyone will have a nametag, and will be invited by the host to share their thoughts in the seminars. Such activity format was inspired by US liberal arts based education, valuing interdisciplinary, freedom of expression and belonging. Apart from weekly activities, ETO also held projects. In 2024, we attempted to invite the book's author, Mr. Cixin Liu, to give speech at SCIE, but received a kind rejection. Last year, we successfully invited the department head of Astronomy of Sun Yat-sen Univeristy, Prof. Jun Cui, to speak at SCIE, making tangible community impact. Next year, we will be focusing on publishing and creating an archive to the content of our discussion

## 社团介绍

The Theoretical Physics Club is a student-led academic ECA designed to introduce students to fundamental physics that can help consolidate their background knowledge. We will not delve into very complicated topics, and members are only required to know P1 Calculus. Using Physics for Scientists and Engineers (6th Edition) by Paul A. Tipler and Gene Mosca as our main textbook, we explore fundamental concepts through a combination of guided teaching, mathematical derivations, conceptual discussions, and challenging problem-solving. The club will cover major areas of physics including mechanics, oscillations and waves, thermodynamics, electromagnetism, optics, and introductory modern physics. Sessions will be interactive, where members are encouraged to discuss and challenge each other with their own understandings. It will operate more likely as a study group rather than lecture-based classes.

## 社团介绍

EduSpark is a student-led education and social impact ECA that aims to make learning more engaging, accessible, and connected to the wider world. Members explore real-world topics such as culture, history, economics, technology, mathematics, sustainability, and global inequality, and transform what they learn into interactive educational activities. Rather than simply volunteering as tutors, members will work collaboratively to design discussion-based lessons, educational games, workshops, and outreach projects for younger students and communities with fewer opportunities to access diverse perspectives. The ECA is built around four principles: curiosity, critical thinking, global perspective, and student empowerment. Members will develop their own ideas, test activities, receive feedback, and improve them through reflection. Our work will include both on-campus educational activities and community outreach, including the EduSpark Shanxi teaching project. Through these experiences, we hope to create a sustainable cycle: students learn, students design, students teach, and students create positive social impact. Ultimately, EduSpark aims to help both our members and the students we reach see a bigger world, think more deeply, and believe that they can make a difference.

## 社团介绍

The Electromagnetics & Optics Society is a theory‑oriented club focused on the fundamental principles of electricity, magnetism, and light. Our sessions cover topics such as Ohm’s law, electric fields, potential energy, Lorentz force, magnetic flux, electromagnetic induction, Lenz’s law, motional and induced EMF, photoelectric effect, double‑slit interference, grating diffraction, and half‑wave loss. We meet weekly for guided discussions and problem‑solving sessions. The emphasis is on building a clear conceptual understanding and working through challenging questions together. Simple demonstrations may be included occasionally to help visualize abstract ideas, but the main focus remains on theoretical reasoning and group discussion. Members are encouraged to ask questions and share their thinking. All students with an interest in physics are welcome, regardless of prior background.

- Andy — Student s24458.zhang@stu.scie.com.cn

## 社团介绍

The Entrepreneurship Club aims to introduce students to both the theoretical and practical sides of entrepreneurship. Inspired by my experience at LaunchX, where I studied entrepreneurial concepts and developed a startup with my team, I wanted to create a space at school for students who are interested in business but may not yet have opportunities to explore entrepreneurship in practice. Through interactive workshops, case discussions, and group projects, members learn how to identify market opportunities, conduct market research, develop value propositions and MVPs, understand basic business models and finance, and pitch ideas effectively. Rather than focusing only on lectures, the club encourages students to apply these concepts to real-world problems. For example, members have discussed developing a platform that connects food-delivery services while providing calorie information, personalized dietary planning, and restaurant recommendations for users with fitness goals. Ultimately, the club hopes to help students become more observant, creative, and analytical problem-solvers while gaining practical experience in turning an initial idea into a viable business concept.

| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Great Books Society is a student-led reading and discussion club for students interested in philosophy, literature, history, politics, and the fundamental questions of human life. Each week, members will read a short text or selected excerpts from an influential work and discuss a central question raised by it: What is justice? What makes a good life? Are we truly free? What do we owe to others? What makes political authority legitimate? Possible readings will include works by Plato, Aristotle, Confucius, Shakespeare, John Stuart Mill, Nietzsche, Hannah Arendt, and other major thinkers and writers from different intellectual traditions. No previous knowledge of philosophy is required, and readings will be kept manageable and accessible. Meetings will combine close reading, guided discussion, short debates, thought experiments, and student-led presentations. Rather than looking for one “correct” interpretation, members will learn to question assumptions, examine arguments, listen carefully to different perspectives, and develop their own reasoned responses to enduring human questions.

| Category | ACADEMIC Others |

| Students | 1 |
| Period | Monday Period13 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

GreenRoot Society is a student-led community dedicated to exploring the connections between environmental change and human health—starting with soil. Our journey begins with personal story from Fujian, where agricultural pollution have harmed local farmland, showing the hidden urgency of soil protection. Members will then participate in the Out of Eden Learn – Introduction to Planetary Health learning journey, inspired by Paul Salopek’s slow journalism walk around the world. Through four “Footsteps,” we will: 1. Observe – Take mindful walks in our communities to notice and record environmental and health-related changes. 2. Connect – Build concept maps linking environmental changes such as pollution or biodiversity loss with specific human health impacts. 3. Investigate – Focus on one planetary health issue, research it through observation, interviews, and reliable resources, and share findings. 4. Act – Identify daily choices that affect planetary health and create advocacy resources to inspire change. Through GreenRoot Society, students will combine observation, scientific thinking, and storytelling to become thoughtful, informed advocates for the planet—proving that local actions can contribute to global health.

## 社团介绍

Humanities and Female Lens are two key words for this ECA. Humanities play a crucial role in our lives, especially in the world we now live in. Without literature, history, or philosophy, STEM would become lifeless numbers. Therefore, recognizing the importance of the humanities and how it influences our world is essential for personal and societal growth. In order to understand humanities, we have to approach it with different perspectives. Although there are several ECAs that focus on humanities, this ECA stands out as unique because it offers knowledge through a new, female perspective. Due to gender inequality, many influential women have been overlooked, and their contributions often went unrecognised. ‘Hermanity’ aims to help students appreciate the value of the humanities through a female lens and foster students’ critical thinking, collaboration, and public speaking skills. We thrive to reverse the stereotypes linked with humanities and overcome difficulties in reading and writing. Overall, we aim to foster student’s intrinsic motivations by giving members the opportunities to learn, and offer them a new lens when viewing our world.

## 社团介绍

This ECA uses art and storytelling to explore the imperfections, challenges, and unexpected moments that shape people’s lives. This year, its main focus will be elderly care and the experiences of older adults, using creative expression to encourage students to look beyond stereotypes of aging and understand the people behind them. Students will visit elderly-care communities and related facilities, where they will observe daily life, conduct interviews, and explore topics such as aging, memories, family relationships, loneliness, independence, dignity, and the changing relationship between generations. They will work together to create a documentary that records these experiences and gives elderly participants an opportunity to share their own stories and perspectives. Alongside documentary filmmaking, students will transform what they learn into different forms of art, including photography, painting, writing, illustration, and installation. These works will explore themes such as memory, aging, vulnerability, resilience, and human connection. The creative process will encourage students to reflect on their own assumptions about aging while developing empathy, communication, and storytelling skills. The ECA will culminate in an exhibition combining student artwork, documentary footage, photographs, and other materials collected throughout the project. By bringing these stories to a wider audience, the exhibition will encourage conversations about elderly care and intergenerational understanding, ultimately turning individual stories and everyday experiences into meaningful narratives of connection.

Classroom: B531

## 社团介绍

InvestNow aims to make finance accessible, practical, and intellectually engaging, especially for students with little or no prior experience. Through interactive lessons, real-world cases, portfolio simulations, and discussions of current financial news, members will gradually develop the ability to understand businesses, interpret basic financial information, evaluate risk and return, and form their own investment opinions. The semester will culminate in a stock pitch, where students apply what they have learned to research, analyze, and defend an investment idea. Following feedback from previous members, we have redesigned our curriculum to provide a much stronger foundation before introducing advanced material. In addition, our experienced academic team, with strong results in national and international economics and investment competitions, also offers optional advanced sessions covering topics such as DCF valuation, portfolio and risk modelling, and derivatives. Beyond weekly teaching, InvestNow has already developed into a wider platform for financial education and student collaboration. We have organized two inter-school investment competitions involving students from schools spanning Suzhou to Shenzhen, collaborated with the Global Citizenship team to present social-enterprise projects during Sustainability Week, and have been invited to serve as one of the organizing societies for a seven-school Greater Bay Area investment competition.

The French Film ECA is a space for students to watch and discuss a wide range of French-language films together. Through films from different genres, periods, and French-speaking regions, members will explore French culture, society, history, and cinematic aesthetics. After each film, members will be encouraged to share their personal views and interpretations in a friendly and open discussion. We believe that listening to different opinions can help us notice details and perspectives that we may have missed on our own. Through these discussions, we hope to develop a deeper appreciation of cinema and a more thoughtful way of viewing the world. The ECA welcomes everyone, regardless of their level of French or previous knowledge of film. English subtitles will be provided when necessary. Our main aim is to enjoy French-language cinema together while learning from the films and from one another.

## 社团介绍

The Theoretical Physics Club is a student-led academic ECA designed to introduce students to fundamental physics that can help consolidate their background knowledge. We will not delve into very complicated topics, and members are only required to know P1 Calculus. Using Physics for Scientists and Engineers (6th Edition) by Paul A. Tipler and Gene Mosca as our main textbook, we explore fundamental concepts through a combination of guided teaching, mathematical derivations, conceptual discussions, and challenging problem-solving. The club will cover major areas of physics including mechanics, oscillations and waves, thermodynamics, electromagnetism, optics, and introductory modern physics. Sessions will be interactive, where members are encouraged to discuss and challenge each other with their own understandings. It will operate more likely as a study group rather than lecture-based classes.

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5200/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| Leader |  |

| Category | ACADEMIC Languages |
| Room | A211 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Great Books Society is a student-led reading and discussion club for students interested in philosophy, literature, history, politics, and the fundamental questions of human life. Each week, members will read a short text or selected excerpts from an influential work and discuss a central question raised by it: What is justice? What makes a good life? Are we truly free? What do we owe to others? What makes political authority legitimate? Possible readings will include works by Plato, Aristotle, Confucius, Shakespeare, John Stuart Mill, Nietzsche, Hannah Arendt, and other major thinkers and writers from different intellectual traditions. No previous knowledge of philosophy is required, and readings will be kept manageable and accessible. Meetings will combine close reading, guided discussion, short debates, thought experiments, and student-led presentations. Rather than looking for one “correct” interpretation, members will learn to question assumptions, examine arguments, listen carefully to different perspectives, and develop their own reasoned responses to enduring human questions.

- Kira — Student s25027.li@stu.scie.com.cn

Classroom: A211

# GreenRoot Society

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5054/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 73 |
| Name | GreenRoot Society |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 23309 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Others |
| Room | A506 |
| Students | 3 |
| Period | Monday Period13 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

GreenRoot Society is a student-led community dedicated to exploring the connections between environmental change and human health—starting with soil. Our journey begins with personal story from Fujian, where agricultural pollution have harmed local farmland, showing the hidden urgency of soil protection. Members will then participate in the Out of Eden Learn – Introduction to Planetary Health learning journey, inspired by Paul Salopek’s slow journalism walk around the world. Through four “Footsteps,” we will: 1. Observe – Take mindful walks in our communities to notice and record environmental and health-related changes. 2. Connect – Build concept maps linking environmental changes such as pollution or biodiversity loss with specific human health impacts. 3. Investigate – Focus on one planetary health issue, research it through observation, interviews, and reliable resources, and share findings. 4. Act – Identify daily choices that affect planetary health and create advocacy resources to inspire change. Through GreenRoot Society, students will combine observation, scientific thinking, and storytelling to become thoughtful, informed advocates for the planet—proving that local actions can contribute to global health.

## 负责人

- Jeremy — Student s23309.han@stu.scie.com.cn

Time: Period 13 (Random time after school)
Classroom: A506

# Mathematical and Physical Society

| Category | ACADEMIC Natural sciences |
| Room | B421 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Our society is a student-run academic group focused on mathematics and physics, operating within our school. The core activity is simple: we invite experienced members—senior students who have a solid grasp of particular topics—to give regular walk-through sessions on standard textbooks, helping fellow students navigate difficult derivations, solve problems step by step, and clarify common misconceptions. Unlike formal lectures, our sessions are interactive and discussion-friendly; attendees are encouraged to interrupt with questions whenever something is unclear. We believe that peer-to-peer explanation, coming from someone who recently struggled with the same material, is often the most accessible bridge between a dense textbook and genuine understanding. Beyond our weekly sessions, we maintain a small public account where we share concise reading notes, worked examples, and occasional popular-science pieces. We might also collaborate with the school's sci-fi club on informal joint meetups in the future, where we pick a scientific idea from a novel or film and walk through how plausible it really is under known physics—just for fun and curiosity. Our society is not about grand ambitions; it is about building a friendly, low-pressure space where students who enjoy math and physics can share what they know, help each other learn, and maybe discover that these two subjects are less intimidating when tackled together.

- Thomas — Student s25366.liu@stu.scie.com.cn

Time: Period 11 (First period after school)
Classroom: B421

## 社团介绍

HugS(Helping Unique Growing Souls) is determined to provide help for the children with physical disabilities by multiple methods, for example, advocacy, education, and hands-on volunteer activities. Children with disabilities include those with physical disabilities, intellectual or cognitive disorders, and neurodevelopmental disorders such as autism spectrum disorder. Although awareness of providing special education to those children has improved in recent years, the phenomenon of a lack of public understanding, social stigma, and limited opportunities for accessing inclusive education persists. As a result, the primary aim of our club would be to improve their situation in society in the long term and provide common knowledge of them to those with limited understanding. By collaborating with local welfare centers, we want to provide more meaningful social engagement for students and the community so that they are able to engage with these special children and understand their special situation and lived experiences. Members are also encouraged to participate in awareness campaigns and the promotion of special education.

| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Humanities and Female Lens are two key words for this ECA. Humanities play a crucial role in our lives, especially in the world we now live in. Without literature, history, or philosophy, STEM would become lifeless numbers. Therefore, recognizing the importance of the humanities and how it influences our world is essential for personal and societal growth. In order to understand humanities, we have to approach it with different perspectives. Although there are several ECAs that focus on humanities, this ECA stands out as unique because it offers knowledge through a new, female perspective. Due to gender inequality, many influential women have been overlooked, and their contributions often went unrecognised. ‘Hermanity’ aims to help students appreciate the value of the humanities through a female lens and foster students’ critical thinking, collaboration, and public speaking skills. We thrive to reverse the stereotypes linked with humanities and overcome difficulties in reading and writing. Overall, we aim to foster student’s intrinsic motivations by giving members the opportunities to learn, and offer them a new lens when viewing our world.

Model United Nations (MUN) at SCIE offers students an engaging platform to explore global affairs, diplomacy, and international relations. Designed as an academic and lecture-based ECA, weekly sessions provide structured guidance on geopolitics, parliamentary procedure inspired by the United Nations, resolution drafting, and effective public speaking. Members gain a comprehensive understanding of how international bodies operate while analyzing real-world global challenges. In addition to weekly instructional sessions, members have the opportunity to represent our school at external MUN conferences, applying their skills in competitive, multi-school debates. Furthermore, club members actively participate in organizing, planning, and hosting our school's annual MUN conference—a major event that fosters leadership, logistics management, and teamwork. Whether you are a beginner looking to build confidence or an experienced delegate aiming to refine your diplomatic skills, this ECA equips you with critical thinking and leadership abilities for the future.

Integrated Strategies Strategies Integrated is an ECA that explores how history can be learned, represented and debated through video games, board games and strategy games. Members will look at how games set in different periods, such as Ancient Rome, medieval kingdoms, the World Wars or the Cold War, present historical events, leaders, societies and conflicts. ## 社团介绍

InvestNow aims to make finance accessible, practical, and intellectually engaging, especially for students with little or no prior experience. Through interactive lessons, real-world cases, portfolio simulations, and discussions of current financial news, members will gradually develop the ability to understand businesses, interpret basic financial information, evaluate risk and return, and form their own investment opinions. The semester will culminate in a stock pitch, where students apply what they have learned to research, analyze, and defend an investment idea. Following feedback from previous members, we have redesigned our curriculum to provide a much stronger foundation before introducing advanced material. In addition, our experienced academic team, with strong results in national and international economics and investment competitions, also offers optional advanced sessions covering topics such as DCF valuation, portfolio and risk modelling, and derivatives. Beyond weekly teaching, InvestNow has already developed into a wider platform for financial education and student collaboration. We have organized two inter-school investment competitions involving students from schools spanning Suzhou to Shenzhen, collaborated with the Global Citizenship team to present social-enterprise projects during Sustainability Week, and have been invited to serve as one of the organizing societies for a seven-school Greater Bay Area investment competition.

## 社团介绍

The National History ECA will focus on preparing the students for the National History Day competition which is usually held in March at Concordia International School Shanghai. In preparation, students will conduct their own research and create a website on the Historical theme of 'Innovation in History: Impact, Influence, Change.' Students will then present their research to a panel of judges on the day of the competition. Winners of the initial competition will have the opportunity to display their projects in the finals in the United States! The ECA will provide an introduction to the NHD competition, and ongoing support for your project

## 社团介绍

JGG is a music club built on joy, energy, and good vibes. The name comes from the Chinese “Jiao Gao Gao,” meaning “standing on tiptoes and reaching higher.” It also stands for “Just Get Groovy.” We are a group of music lovers who enjoy playing, creating, and jamming together. Our style blends rock, pop, and indie influences. We rehearse regularly, write original songs, and perform at campus and city events. We believe making music should be fun, not stressful. Whether you play an instrument or just love to sing and listen, everyone is welcome. Join JGG, get groovy, and turn the volume up!

## 社团介绍

The Nature History Club is an ECA in the Natural Science category. Students interested in biological science and the history of science meet up once a week to explore topics beyond the textbook’s level in depth. Usually, the ECA leaders give lectures to the students around a certain biology-related area (e.g., Applied Evolution) and the history of science, while animated videos and fun questions are included to make the lecture more intriguing. There will sometimes be extra activities such as biological experiments taken into practice occasionally during the ECA’s activity time. The Nature History Club is also involved in constructing the SCIE Garden and Greenhouse. We spend a couple of ECA sessions per semester to work on the SCIE Greenhouse, aiming to study plants habitats, investigate plant growing techniques, and plant seeds by ourselves in the SCIE Greenhouse. Our efforts on the greenhouse will be recorded in our greenhouse journals, which might be published in the future. The Nature History Club encourages students to voice out their own thoughts and be the lecturer. We will spare several lessons for these lecturer students.

# Nice Calculus

| 字段 | 内容 |

| Leader | HAK |

| Category | ACADEMIC Mathematics and computer sciences |
| Room | B523 |
| Students | 4 |
| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

"Nice Calculus" is the newly rebranded continuation of our previous "Calculus" ECA. Derived from the names of its co-leaders, Niko and Bruce, the name "Nice" reflects our club's core educational philosophy. This year, we go beyond teaching rigorous calculus concepts and advanced techniques; our primary focus is helping students develop a profound mathematical intuition—a crucial skill often overlooked in traditional high school curriculums. We will achieve this through a highly visual teaching style, utilizing custom-programmed mathematics animations (such as those built with Python's Manim) and interactive visualization applications. By stepping away from purely algebraic problem-solving, we aim to demonstrate that sensing math geometrically, physically, and visually strengthens independent thinking and fundamentally transforms how students understand the nature of mathematics. Furthermore, based on feedback from previous terms, we have significantly upgraded our syllabus. We are extending our lessons to include applied mathematics topics, such as the multivariable calculus essential for higher-level physics and engineering. By pairing these advanced concepts with concrete examples and visual tools, we ensure our members gain a comprehensive, well-rounded mastery of mathematics.

## 社团介绍

Life Pawnshop is an ECA focusing on scientific self-exploration through cognitive psychology and community building. Instead of relying on superficial and changeable personality tests such as MBTI, this club uses psychological theories, cognitive bias experiments, value-choice games and movie analysis to help students identify what combination of personality traits makes them who they are, inner goals they might not have recognized yet, and subconscious preferences. I hope to create a safe space for students to “drop the mask”, see their true selves, and learn to build a psychological defense system against external stressors. At Life Pawnshop, we will try to be honest, even if that means we are unsure, confused, and even awkward. But we will walk out braver and more confident.

Club members solve selected, inspiring problems together on online judge platforms (e.g. Codeforces, Luogu); specific problems are chosen to best reveal how intriguing the subject is. The club also focuses on highlighting the fascinating intersection between mathematics and computer science, for example how computers could aid counting problems and logic verification. # Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence Club (MLxAI)

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5228/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 91 |
| Name | Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence Club (MLxAI) |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 23291 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Mathematics and computer sciences |
| Room | B433 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

SCIE ML×AI is a hands-on AI and data science ECA open to students with or without prior machine-learning experience. It develops members through three connected areas: Data Science, using Kaggle competitions to learn data analysis, validation, modelling and submission; AI Olympiads, using NOAI/IOAI-style problems to build deep-learning intuition and problem-solving insight; and Agentic Engineering, using prompts(a.k.a Agentic Skills), coding agents and multi-agent systems to implement AI models efficiently. No prior experience with coding is required as members will not copy code line by line. AI tools handle much of the implementation, while students define tasks, run experiments, evaluate results and decide how to improve their models. Seven guided sessions establish shared foundations in agent setup, tabular modelling, NLP, computer vision, Transformers and multimodal learning. The ECA then continues as a collaborative problem-solving lab using real datasets and competition tasks. Members may work independently or in teams, prepare competition submissions and present their methods, results and lessons to the school community.

This Paper Quilling ECA is a creative art community dedicated to the traditional craft of paper quilling — the art of rolling, shaping and gluing thin paper strips to form delicate decorative artworks. We welcome students of all skill levels, from complete beginners to those with prior crafting experience. In our sessions, members will master fundamental quilling techniques and create a wide range of works, including bookmarks, greeting cards, wall art and 3D miniatures. Beyond hands-on creation, the club integrates creative expression, aesthetic appreciation and public welfare. We will organise themed creation projects and donate selected works to community charity programs. Our club aims to provide a relaxing, supportive space for students to unwind from academic pressure, nurture patience and fine motor skills, and connect with peers who share a passion for handmade art.

# Pause.

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5132/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |

| Leader | AMS |

| Category | SOCIAL Interests and hobbies |
| Room | G021 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Tuesday LunchTime |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

‘Pause.’ is a community designed to make mindfulness practical, accessible, and relevant to everyday student life. Rather than treating mindfulness as simply “sitting still and meditating,” the club explores how attention, awareness, and intentional reflection can influence the way we study, communicate, manage stress, and experience everyday moments. Each session combines a short guided mindfulness practice with an interactive activity or discussion. Students may explore breathing and grounding exercises, mindful movement, attention training, gratitude, mindful listening, digital awareness, and reflection. No previous experience is required, and students are encouraged to participate at their own comfort level. The goal is not to eliminate stress or constantly “feel positive,” but to develop the ability to notice our thoughts, emotions, and surroundings without immediately reacting to them. Through regular practice, Pause. aims to help students become more attentive to themselves and the people around them.

# Physics and Philosophy

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5111/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |

| Leader | HAJ |

| Category | ACADEMIC Natural sciences |
| Room | A519 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Physics and Philosophy ECA is a student-led interdisciplinary club exploring the conceptual foundations behind scientific theories and their implications for humanity. While physics provides models to describe the universe, philosophy helps us examine the assumptions, interpretations, and consequences behind those models. The club will explore topics including the interpretation of quantum mechanics (such as the measurement problem and Schrödinger’s Cat), the nature of space and time in Einstein’s relativity, the philosophical foundations of scientific methods, and ethical questions arising from technologies such as artificial intelligence and genetic engineering. Activities will include guided discussions, student presentations, thought experiments, debates, and analysis of scientific papers or philosophical texts. Members will be encouraged to move beyond memorising theories and instead ask deeper questions: Why do scientific models work? What does it mean for a theory to represent reality? How should society respond to new technologies? The ECA welcomes students interested in physics, philosophy, mathematics, humanities, or simply exploring challenging ideas through logical reasoning and open discussion.

- Nico — Student s23502.liao@stu.scie.com.cn

# Math-Physics Exploration

| Students | 1 |
| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Physics–Math Exploration is a student-led academic club for students who would like to explore how mathematical ideas become tools for understanding physical systems. Starting from familiar classroom topics such as motion, forces, waves and energy, each session will begin with a modelling question(near our learning curriculum): What should we represent? Which assumptions are reasonable? What can the mathematics predict, and where does the model stop working? Sessions will combine short explanations(trying with both languages), guided discussion, and one or two carefully chosen problems(for each session). Useful mathematical tools—including vectors, trigonometry, parameterisation, graphs, introductory calculus ideas and data analysis—will be introduced when they become useful, rather than as isolated techniques. The aim is not simply to move ahead through a syllabus, but to develop curiosity, precise reasoning and confidence in turning everyday questions into solvable physical models. Students with different levels of mathematical experience are welcome; optional extension problems and small projects will provide further challenge.

## 社团介绍

Football is far more than a sport—it is a magnifying glass for history, politics, social class, migration, and identity. From El Clásico in Spain to the Superclásico in Argentina, from the Milan Derby to the North West Derby in England, every classic rivalry carries within it decades or even centuries of cultural tension and collective memory. Our ECA is dedicated to unearthing these stories. The Football Culture Studies Club is a student-led organization that aims to go beyond scores and highlights, delving deeply into the historical roots, social conflicts, and fan cultures behind the world's famous derbies. Through weekly topic sharing, guided discussions, and member-led research projects, we seek to understand: why does football mean so much to people across the globe?

## 社团介绍

Mathematical Structures Society (MSS) is a student-led mathematics exploration community that aims to help students discover the deeper ideas behind mathematics beyond the standard curriculum. Rather than focusing only on competition training or solving individual problems, MSS encourages students to explore how mathematical ideas are connected through patterns, structures, and logical reasoning. Through workshops, discussions, problem-solving sessions, and student presentations, members will gradually develop the ability to move from concrete problems toward abstract mathematical thinking. The club will introduce accessible topics from areas such as mathematical reasoning, combinatorics, number theory, symmetry, graph theory, cryptography, and introductory abstract algebra. By connecting mathematics with real-world applications, art, technology, and everyday phenomena, MSS hopes to make advanced mathematical thinking approachable for high school students.

# Mathematical and Physical Society

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5092/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 100 |
| Name | Mathematical and Physical Society |
| Leader |  |
| Leadership Team | 25366 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Natural sciences |
| Room | B421 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Our society is a student-run academic group focused on mathematics and physics, operating within our school. The core activity is simple: we invite experienced members—senior students who have a solid grasp of particular topics—to give regular walk-through sessions on standard textbooks, helping fellow students navigate difficult derivations, solve problems step by step, and clarify common misconceptions. Unlike formal lectures, our sessions are interactive and discussion-friendly; attendees are encouraged to interrupt with questions whenever something is unclear. We believe that peer-to-peer explanation, coming from someone who recently struggled with the same material, is often the most accessible bridge between a dense textbook and genuine understanding. Beyond our weekly sessions, we maintain a small public account where we share concise reading notes, worked examples, and occasional popular-science pieces. We might also collaborate with the school's sci-fi club on informal joint meetups in the future, where we pick a scientific idea from a novel or film and walk through how plausible it really is under known physics—just for fun and curiosity. Our society is not about grand ambitions; it is about building a friendly, low-pressure space where students who enjoy math and physics can share what they know, help each other learn, and maybe discover that these two subjects are less intimidating when tackled together.

- Thomas — Student s25366.liu@stu.scie.com.cn

## 社团介绍

MindBridge is a peer-driven forum for students passionate about mental health and clinical science. We explore core topics including psychopathology, evidence-based interventions, diagnostic systems, ethics, and the scientist‑practitioner model. Through interactive case discussions, journal article reviews, role‑play demonstrations, and occasional guest speakers, members gain a realistic preview of clinical psychology training and practice. Our sessions foster critical thinking, cultural humility, and the integration of research with real‑world clinical work. We aim to build a supportive community where curiosity about human behaviour meets rigorous scientific inquiry, helping members prepare for further study or careers in mental health. The ECA values diversity, empathy, and evidence‑based approaches, while also encouraging self‑reflection and personal growth. We welcome anyone interested in understanding psychological distress and healing, regardless of prior background, and we strive to reduce stigma around mental health through open, respectful dialogue.

- Susan — Student s23551.sang@stu.scie.com.cn

Under the guidance of Shenzhen Red Cross headquarters and school medical teachers, we integrate first aid training, charity volunteer activities and mental health promotion. We hold regular after-school activities every Tuesday, providing systematic first aid knowledge learning, forming first aid teams for school sports events, carrying out volunteer services at Min'ai Welfare Institute, organizing AHA first aid certificate training, Mental Health Week and first aid lectures. ## 社团介绍

Resonance is a discussion-based ECA dedicated to exploring the deeper ideologies, political messages, and social representations embedded within popular and alternative music. Far from just listening to catchy tunes, we analyze music as a historical and cultural artifact. We will dissect how artists use their platforms to express the struggles of specific demographics, challenge the status quo, and reflect the historical zeitgeist of their eras. For example, we will analyze Green Day's American Idiot as a critique of post-9/11 media paranoia, exploring who the "idiot" represents and what generational anxieties the album voices. By bridging music with social sciences and history, members will develop critical media literacy, learn to interpret complex narratives, and enjoy lively, respectful debates about the soundtracks that shape our society.

## 社团介绍

Model United Nations (MUN) at SCIE offers students an engaging platform to explore global affairs, diplomacy, and international relations. Designed as an academic and lecture-based ECA, weekly sessions provide structured guidance on geopolitics, parliamentary procedure inspired by the United Nations, resolution drafting, and effective public speaking. Members gain a comprehensive understanding of how international bodies operate while analyzing real-world global challenges. In addition to weekly instructional sessions, members have the opportunity to represent our school at external MUN conferences, applying their skills in competitive, multi-school debates. Furthermore, club members actively participate in organizing, planning, and hosting our school's annual MUN conference—a major event that fosters leadership, logistics management, and teamwork. Whether you are a beginner looking to build confidence or an experienced delegate aiming to refine your diplomatic skills, this ECA equips you with critical thinking and leadership abilities for the future.

The SCIE Champion League (SCL) is an inclusive, intramural 6-a-side football league dedicated to fostering sportsmanship, teamwork, and healthy competition within the school community. Designed to welcome students of all genders and skill levels—from absolute beginners to advanced players—SCL prioritizes passion and creativity over technical perfection, providing a highly supportive environment for athletic expression. A defining feature of the league is its NBA-inspired draft mechanism. At the beginning of the academic year, all participants undergo a skills assessment followed by a structured draft, ensuring balanced team rosters and highly competitive midday matches. Beyond intramural play, SCL also unites its top talents into a representative squad to compete in friendly matches against other international schools. More than just a sports tournament, SCL acts as a dynamic campus platform that bridges diverse student groups, cultivates school spirit, and promotes holistic development through a shared love of the beautiful game.

## 社团介绍

The Nature History Club is an ECA in the Natural Science category. Students interested in biological science and the history of science meet up once a week to explore topics beyond the textbook’s level in depth. Usually, the ECA leaders give lectures to the students around a certain biology-related area (e.g., Applied Evolution) and the history of science, while animated videos and fun questions are included to make the lecture more intriguing. There will sometimes be extra activities such as biological experiments taken into practice occasionally during the ECA’s activity time. The Nature History Club is also involved in constructing the SCIE Garden and Greenhouse. We spend a couple of ECA sessions per semester to work on the SCIE Greenhouse, aiming to study plants habitats, investigate plant growing techniques, and plant seeds by ourselves in the SCIE Greenhouse. Our efforts on the greenhouse will be recorded in our greenhouse journals, which might be published in the future. The Nature History Club encourages students to voice out their own thoughts and be the lecturer. We will spare several lessons for these lecturer students.

# Nice Calculus

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5090/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 113 |
| Name | Nice Calculus |
| Leader | HAK |
| Leadership Team | 24049 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Mathematics and computer sciences |
| Room | B523 |
| Students | 4 |
| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

"Nice Calculus" is the newly rebranded continuation of our previous "Calculus" ECA. Derived from the names of its co-leaders, Niko and Bruce, the name "Nice" reflects our club's core educational philosophy. This year, we go beyond teaching rigorous calculus concepts and advanced techniques; our primary focus is helping students develop a profound mathematical intuition—a crucial skill often overlooked in traditional high school curriculums. We will achieve this through a highly visual teaching style, utilizing custom-programmed mathematics animations (such as those built with Python's Manim) and interactive visualization applications. By stepping away from purely algebraic problem-solving, we aim to demonstrate that sensing math geometrically, physically, and visually strengthens independent thinking and fundamentally transforms how students understand the nature of mathematics. Furthermore, based on feedback from previous terms, we have significantly upgraded our syllabus. We are extending our lessons to include applied mathematics topics, such as the multivariable calculus essential for higher-level physics and engineering. By pairing these advanced concepts with concrete examples and visual tools, we ensure our members gain a comprehensive, well-rounded mastery of mathematics.

This Paper Quilling ECA is a creative art community dedicated to the traditional craft of paper quilling — the art of rolling, shaping and gluing thin paper strips to form delicate decorative artworks. We welcome students of all skill levels, from complete beginners to those with prior crafting experience. In our sessions, members will master fundamental quilling techniques and create a wide range of works, including bookmarks, greeting cards, wall art and 3D miniatures. Beyond hands-on creation, the club integrates creative expression, aesthetic appreciation and public welfare. We will organise themed creation projects and donate selected works to community charity programs. Our club aims to provide a relaxing, supportive space for students to unwind from academic pressure, nurture patience and fine motor skills, and connect with peers who share a passion for handmade art.

# Pause.

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5132/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |

| Leader | AMS |
