| Category | SOCIAL Interests and hobbies |
| Room | G021 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Tuesday LunchTime |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

‘Pause.’ is a community designed to make mindfulness practical, accessible, and relevant to everyday student life. Rather than treating mindfulness as simply “sitting still and meditating,” the club explores how attention, awareness, and intentional reflection can influence the way we study, communicate, manage stress, and experience everyday moments. Each session combines a short guided mindfulness practice with an interactive activity or discussion. Students may explore breathing and grounding exercises, mindful movement, attention training, gratitude, mindful listening, digital awareness, and reflection. No previous experience is required, and students are encouraged to participate at their own comfort level. The goal is not to eliminate stress or constantly “feel positive,” but to develop the ability to notice our thoughts, emotions, and surroundings without immediately reacting to them. Through regular practice, Pause. aims to help students become more attentive to themselves and the people around them.

## 社团介绍

SCIE Philosophy Club is a discussion-based ECA for students interested in examining everyday questions more carefully. Each session begins with a question, thought experiment, short reading, film clip, or real-world case, followed by student-led discussion and argument. Topics may include free will, personal identity, morality, knowledge, language, aesthetics, political philosophy, and questions raised by new technologies such as artificial intelligence. Rather than requiring previous knowledge of philosophy, the club will introduce philosophical ideas when they become useful for understanding the question at hand. Members will learn to identify assumptions, construct arguments, respond to objections, and revise their views through discussion. We hope to create an open environment in which disagreement is treated as something to investigate rather than something that has to be resolved immediately.

## 社团介绍

Puzzle Hunt is an event where teams compete to solve a series of puzzles. It originated from MIT in 1981 and has now become one of the most popular events in universities. Puzzle Hunt is a community with diversity. Everyone can find their excellent field. In SCIE Puzzle Hunter, students will delve into different types of puzzles: logic puzzles, crosswords, cryptography, steganography, detective fiction, linguistics, etc. Through this process, we will explore useful techniques that can be used in puzzle hunt: searching, frequency analysis, brute force machines, solvers, etc. We will try past puzzle hunts like MIT Mystery Hunt and Microsoft Puzzle Hunt to feel the charm of those exciting puzzle structures and designs. Furthermore, we will form teams to participate in those ongoing hunts and compete with teams from all over the world. Like those hunts held by other universities, our ultimate goal is to write our own SCIE Puzzle Hunt in this year.

# Physics and Philosophy

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5111/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |

| NO. | 123 |
| Name | Physics and Philosophy |
| Leader | HAJ |
| Leadership Team | 23502 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Natural sciences |
| Room | A519 |
| Students | 2 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Physics and Philosophy ECA is a student-led interdisciplinary club exploring the conceptual foundations behind scientific theories and their implications for humanity. While physics provides models to describe the universe, philosophy helps us examine the assumptions, interpretations, and consequences behind those models. The club will explore topics including the interpretation of quantum mechanics (such as the measurement problem and Schrödinger’s Cat), the nature of space and time in Einstein’s relativity, the philosophical foundations of scientific methods, and ethical questions arising from technologies such as artificial intelligence and genetic engineering. Activities will include guided discussions, student presentations, thought experiments, debates, and analysis of scientific papers or philosophical texts. Members will be encouraged to move beyond memorising theories and instead ask deeper questions: Why do scientific models work? What does it mean for a theory to represent reality? How should society respond to new technologies? The ECA welcomes students interested in physics, philosophy, mathematics, humanities, or simply exploring challenging ideas through logical reasoning and open discussion.

The SCIE Rhythm Games ECA targets players interested in further understanding rhythm games and making friends with similar interests. The ECA focuses on building strong connections between players and educating players on rhythm game chart production. During each session, a designated presenter will provide a short introduction lasting 15 to 30 minutes around a typical type of rhythm game, famous rhythm game music producer, or chart production. Advanced players knowledgeable about the specific topics could volunteer to be the presenter in charge of the introductions for the weeks, which helps improve the quality of content and sense of participation. Members can stay after the introduction to attend activities such as video recording or join multi-player mode with other members. Regular activities such as song producer guessing, draw and guess, and friendly competition amongst club members will also be available. This ECA plans to provide lessons and activities that alternate between different options. The session will last from 4:45 to 5:15. The Game introduction will focus on a famous rhythm game, introducing its mechanism, style of music and art, well-known songs and characters, and strengths and weaknesses. Music producers' introductions will focus on their music groups, how their style and genre changed over time, their best songs, and the famous rhythm game songs they produced. The presenter will present the topic each week with a PowerPoint presentation and short clips of the game and music. Below is a brief draft for the first 10 ECA sessions.

## 社团介绍

Football is far more than a sport—it is a magnifying glass for history, politics, social class, migration, and identity. From El Clásico in Spain to the Superclásico in Argentina, from the Milan Derby to the North West Derby in England, every classic rivalry carries within it decades or even centuries of cultural tension and collective memory. Our ECA is dedicated to unearthing these stories. The Football Culture Studies Club is a student-led organization that aims to go beyond scores and highlights, delving deeply into the historical roots, social conflicts, and fan cultures behind the world's famous derbies. Through weekly topic sharing, guided discussions, and member-led research projects, we seek to understand: why does football mean so much to people across the globe?

The Policy Insight ECA will focus on the subject of public policy. We invite students who are interested in policy, sociology, economics, or just want to dive into the policy around us. Each week, we will select one concrete, relevant case study close to our daily lives, ranging from our school to our community, and even to the whole society, focusing on policy or creating potential policy that might be helpful. ## 社团介绍

SCIE Visual Novel Horizon is an interdisciplinary platform dedicated to exploring visual novels as a dynamic medium uniting interactive literature, visual arts, and digital technology. Guided by our motto, "Visual novels, tech, and explorations beyond the horizon," the club examines the thematic and structural depth of visual novels through multi-faceted critical frameworks, drawing insights from literature, philosophy, psychology, sociology, and ethics. In parallel with literary critique, we deconstruct the technical and creative craftsmanship required to build compelling visual novels. Members share insights into game engine programming, branching narrative structures, artistic styling, and adaptive sound design. SCIE Visual Novel Horizon provides an open, intellectually rigorous stage where students can present their analyses, share developmental techniques, and engage in candid peer debate. By embracing differing viewpoints, agreement, and constructive disagreement, the club cultivates both sharp critical thinkers and capable multimedia creators.

| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

Against the widespread student psychological pressure, this ECA aims to improve students’ health and well-being by targeting the gut-mental health link. Scientific research identifies the gut as the human “second brain”, and imbalanced gut microbiota or impaired gut barrier function can significantly raise stress hormone levels and contribute to anxiety and low mood. Regarding the link between intestinal health and mental wellbeing, this ECA translates science into accessible activities. The ECA aims to raise awareness of nutrition mental health connections via practical exercises and self tracking, thus improving mental wellbeing. In summary, this ECA combines biological knowledge, neuroscience, and psychology with lifestyle management to achieve mental wellbeing via dietary approaches.

# Public-Risk Analysis Club

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5233/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |

| NO. | 131 |
| Name | Public-Risk Analysis Club |
| Leader | SIL |
| Leadership Team | 24520 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Mathematics and computer sciences |
| Room | A415 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

We are a student-led research collective dedicated to dissecting aviation disasters through the lens of public safety. Every session, we reconstruct one historic crash—from cockpit voice recordings to wreckage distribution—and apply the Swiss Cheese Model to trace how minor errors align into catastrophic outcomes. Beyond storytelling, we build our own database: mapping accident hotspots, analyzing pilot age-to-experience correlation (the “U‑curve” of risk), and studying human factors like crew familiarity and circadian rhythms. Our mission is not morbid curiosity, but rational vigilance—transforming tragedy into actionable safety intelligence. We believe every black box holds a lesson not just for aviation, but for emergency response, risk communication, and everyday decision-making. If you seek truth in data and respect in remembrance, join us in rewriting the odds—because the safest flight is the one we never stop learning from.

## 负责人

- Siew — Teacher siew.leh@scie.com.cn
- Harry — Student s24520.wang@stu.scie.com.cn

ReaL People is a student-led game-development community that has been running for two years. During this time, members have worked together to turn ideas into playable experiences through writing, art, design, programming, audio, translation, testing, and production planning. Our major project, Preface, is an original narrative game developed collaboratively through shared work on worldbuilding, branching dialogue, interactive design, character and environment concepts, models, bilingual text, and playtesting. Our club records document this process across pages for story structure, interaction lists, character development, dialogue, translation, and visual assets. This year, we will continue as a practical creative studio where students learn through small team projects, structured feedback, and iterative improvement.

## 社团介绍

SCIE Red Cross, founded in 2010, is one of the oldest and largest societies in Shenzhen College of International Education. It is an outstanding social ECA certified by international organizations, dedicated to promoting humanitarian spirit of borderless care. Under the guidance of Shenzhen Red Cross headquarters and school medical teachers, we integrate first aid training, charity volunteer activities and mental health promotion. We hold regular after-school activities every Tuesday, providing systematic first aid knowledge learning, forming first aid teams for school sports events, carrying out volunteer services at Min'ai Welfare Institute, organizing AHA first aid certificate training, Mental Health Week and first aid lectures. We also launch new projects including first aid handbook compilation and off-campus community first aid teaching, committed to serving the campus and the public with practical humanitarian actions.

## 社团介绍

Resonance is a discussion-based ECA dedicated to exploring the deeper ideologies, political messages, and social representations embedded within popular and alternative music. Far from just listening to catchy tunes, we analyze music as a historical and cultural artifact. We will dissect how artists use their platforms to express the struggles of specific demographics, challenge the status quo, and reflect the historical zeitgeist of their eras. For example, we will analyze Green Day's American Idiot as a critique of post-9/11 media paranoia, exploring who the "idiot" represents and what generational anxieties the album voices. By bridging music with social sciences and history, members will develop critical media literacy, learn to interpret complex narratives, and enjoy lively, respectful debates about the soundtracks that shape our society.

## 社团介绍

The Science, Technology, and Society (STS) Club - "Innovating Today, Transforming Tomorrow" - serves as a dynamic platform for students to explore the intricate relationships between scientific advancements, technological innovations, and their societal impacts. Our mission is to foster critical thinking and interdisciplinary collaboration, empowering students to understand and address contemporary global challenges through the lens of STS. In addition to engaging discussions and research, the club encourages students to form teams and incubate STS-related projects that leverage technology to create positive societal change. These projects may focus on areas such as sustainable development, social equity, ethical technology, and public health. The STS Club aims to cultivate a generation of socially responsible innovators equipped to navigate and shape the future of our interconnected world. Furthermore, the club aspires to publish two issues of an STS journal annually. This initiative will provide students with the opportunity to engage in academic writing, peer review processes, and the publication of original research and essays. By contributing to this journal, members will gain practical experience in scholarly communication and deepen their understanding of the interdisciplinary nature of STS.

## 社团介绍

SCIE Scuba Diving Club is a sports-focused ECA dedicated to promoting scuba diving culture and marine environmental protection. We welcome both PADI-certified divers and ocean-loving students without certification as land volunteers. We run regular sessions on PADI OW foundational theory, diving safety and marine ecosystem knowledge. In summer, we organize certified open-water diving trips to Shenzhen Dapeng, with free equipment including BCD and wetsuits provided. Members can take part in public-welfare dives to clear marine litter, earning community service hours while contributing to ocean conservation. Our club aims to build a community for diving enthusiasts, foster marine protection awareness, and provide safe, well-organized opportunities to explore the underwater world.

## 社团介绍

Silver Impact Initiative is a student-led club dedicated to improving the lives of older adults through science, community action, and youth leadership. Each month, members explore a real-world challenge affecting older adults, learn from scientific research and experts, and develop practical ideas to address the issue. Not only do we aim to help the elderly, we also reflect upon ourselves after identifying the issues of the elderly to learn how to avoid some preventable issues. Our activities follow a four-step model: Learn → Ideate → Act → Reflect. Students may participate in research, volunteering, community projects, design, or innovation. Members also have opportunities to become Project Leaders, where they develop an idea, form a team, coordinate implementation, and measure the project’s outcomes all with the help of resources of the ECA and its members . Projects remain part of the broader Silver Impact Initiative, allowing future students to build upon previous work and creating continuity beyond individual events. Our initial topics include cancer, Alzheimer’s disease, and hearing loss, with a particular focus on developing a long-term hearing-care initiative for older adults. We aim to connect students with appropriate experts, healthcare institutions, community organizations, and nonprofit or foundation resources where possible.

The SCIE Champion League (SCL) is an inclusive, intramural 6-a-side football league dedicated to fostering sportsmanship, teamwork, and healthy competition within the school community. Designed to welcome students of all genders and skill levels—from absolute beginners to advanced players—SCL prioritizes passion and creativity over technical perfection, providing a highly supportive environment for athletic expression. A defining feature of the league is its NBA-inspired draft mechanism. At the beginning of the academic year, all participants undergo a skills assessment followed by a structured draft, ensuring balanced team rosters and highly competitive midday matches. Beyond intramural play, SCL also unites its top talents into a representative squad to compete in friendly matches against other international schools. More than just a sports tournament, SCL acts as a dynamic campus platform that bridges diverse student groups, cultivates school spirit, and promotes holistic development through a shared love of the beautiful game.

## 社团介绍

Skiing Society is a student-run community for skiers and snowboarders of all levels. We are dedicated to creating a supportive, peer-driven environment where members help each other improve, regardless of experience. Every weekend (Saturday or Sunday), the club organises trips to Huafa Indoor Ski Resort in Shenzhen for regular training sessions. Beginners are warmly welcomed—experienced members provide guidance on fundamentals, while more advanced riders share tips on technique and control. Through mutual support and consistent practice, every member can progress safely and confidently. During school holidays, the club also arranges group ski trips to outdoor resorts, offering opportunities to explore real mountains and strengthen friendships beyond campus. Whether you are a complete beginner or an experienced skier, SCIE Ski Club is a place to learn, grow, and share your passion for snow sports. Join us and take your first run with a community that has your back.

The club focuses on developing vocal flexibility, emotional expression, and technical skills—including voice modulation for different character types . SDC encourages collaboration and creativity, allowing students to perform individually or in groups, share feedback, and improve together. ## 社团介绍

The Speedcubing Club is a dynamic and inclusive extracurricular activity dedicated to the art of solving twisty puzzles—most notably the iconic Rubik's Cube—as efficiently as possible. Whether students are complete beginners looking to solve their very first cube or experienced speedcubers aiming to shave seconds off their personal bests, this club provides a supportive and engaging environment for all skill levels. Members will learn foundational solving methods, advanced methods like CFOP (Cross, F2L, OLL, PLL), and optimal finger trick techniques. Beyond just solving puzzles, the club fosters a strong sense of community, encouraging members to share strategies, participate in friendly internal competitions, and celebrate each other's progress. Speedcubing is not just about fast hands; it challenges the mind, builds spatial awareness, and teaches perseverance. Join us to improve your dexterity, challenge your brain, and be part of a vibrant community!

## 社团介绍

Starry Musical aims to provide a platform for discussions between musical lovers and works for the publication of musicals. We welcome every musical lover, or anyone willing to know about musicals. Unlike other musical-related clubs focusing on performing musicals, we mainly create a place for non-singers and non-actors to feel the charm of musicals, which is also a promotion of the thought that “musicals are for everyone as long as you are interested in it”. We will discuss official recordings of musicals, including famous and outstanding foreign ones from Broadway or West End, and more importantly, some Chinese musicals. We want to promote and publicize that there are also good musicals in China, originally produced by Chinese playwrights and Chinese musical actors and actresses. After watching every musical, we plan to showcase our understanding and publicize the musicals by posting videos on social media. Starry Musical channel has already been registered on many platforms online. Everyone in Starry Musical would be responsible for part of the videos, including but not limited to video editing, speakers in the videos and scriptwriters. Also, we plan to link musicals to other topics, like mental illness, depending on the musicals we are watching.

## 社团介绍

The FTC Robotics ECA is a student-led team that designs, builds, and tests a robot for the FIRST Tech Challenge. Members will work together throughout the engineering process, from understanding the competition requirements and developing initial ideas to creating CAD models, manufacturing parts, assembling mechanisms, and testing the completed robot. The ECA will introduce members to practical skills such as CAD, 3D printing, mechanical assembly, tool use, and robot design. Members with different levels of experience are welcome, and more experienced students will help teach and guide newer members. Through hands-on projects, members will learn how individual components work together as part of a complete robotic system. Our main aim is to prepare for the FTC competition while creating a collaborative environment where members can experiment, solve problems, learn from mistakes, and turn their ideas into a working robot.

## 社团介绍

Fitness ECA aims to help students develop a scientific, safe, and sustainable approach to resistance training and physical fitness. Rather than simply exercising together, members will learn the principles behind effective training, including exercise technique, movement mechanics, training progression, recovery, and basic program design. As the ECA leader, I have 3 years of self-weight training and 2 years of systematic resistance-training experience and have developed a deeper understanding of exercise selection, movement patterns, training volume and intensity, fatigue management, and the logic behind different training approaches. I hope to share these ideas in an accessible and practical way, helping members understand not only how to perform an exercise, but also why it is performed in a certain way. Sessions will combine short explanations, practical demonstrations, movement workshops, form analysis, and discussion. The ECA will be suitable for both beginners who want to start training safely and more experienced students who want to improve their understanding of training.

The Grove is a student-led creative community that aims to create a welcoming space where students can connect, explore new experiences and feel free to be themselves. In a busy school environment, students may sometimes feel stressed, disconnected or unsure where they belong. The Grove provides a supportive environment where students can build meaningful connections through shared experiences and collaborative activities. Rather than focusing on one fixed hobby, The Grove encourages members to explore different interests and create experiences together. Activities may include creative workshops, discussions, film sessions, hands-on projects and other ideas proposed by members. While activities can change, the experience remains the same: connecting with others, creating something together and growing as a community. Most importantly, members are not simply participants. They are encouraged to contribute ideas, take responsibility and gradually lead projects. Through this process, students develop communication, collaboration, organization and leadership skills in an authentic environment. Like a grove formed by many individual trees, The Grove grows through the unique identities and contributions of every member.

## 社团介绍

The club focuses on WotaGei, a dynamic Japanese pop dance born out of the glowsticks moves originally for fans to express the support to the people they like on stages. The dancer usually holds two high-brightness glowsticks, and perform intensely, leaving beautiful trails of light in the air. The study of OtaGei, the varient of WotaGei will also be involved in the club activities. Unlike WotaGei, OtaGei uses no glowsticks, and is typically not considered as stage performances. Instead, it is more of a relax meathod for the dancer to immerse and enjoy the background music. For the estimated 20 years since WotaGei has been founded, numerous enthusiasts have been creating new Wotagei choreography, also known as Waza. There will always be new things to learn and perfect in this unique foreign dance.

This is a sport that originated in Switzerland and is now becoming popular in most Asian countries and Regions. This sport is super fun; you won't have any aggressive body contact; instead, all you need to do is work closely with your teammates, act fast, and try your best strategy and angle to attack the net to get points. ## 社团介绍

Our extracurricular activity group is dedicated to creating a safe, open, and comfortable environment where students can freely exchange their unique insights into 3D modeling and explore its practical applications in historical technology, daily life and society. Beyond sharing technical skills, we strive to foster meaningful dialogue among members, encouraging everyone to draw new inspiration and innovative ideas from each other's experiences. We believe learning is a collaborative process, where through active discussion and mutual support, each participant gains valuable knowledge that transcends the software itself. Furthermore, we are committed to translating collective expertise into concrete action. By building a united and collaborative team, we aim to bridge the gap between modeling theory and real-world engineering challenges. Our ultimate goal is to design and implement innovative solutions that meet societal needs—whether it's assistive devices, sustainable design, or community-centered projects. Through this process, we hope to not only enhance our technical skills but also cultivate social responsibility and teamwork, demonstrating that even small-scale creations can have a profound impact on the world around us.

## 社团介绍

The Linguistics Club is a student-led club that explores how language works and how it shapes our understanding of society. Through interactive quizzes, discussions, presentations, and activities, we explore topics including sociolinguistics, language and gender, semantics, phonetics, and language diversity. The club aims to make linguistics accessible and engaging while encouraging students to question assumptions about language and discover how it connects to everyday life.

## 社团介绍

The Cloud Watcher is a scientific observation and study group focused on the atmospheric phenomenon. Members learn to identify and classify clouds and optical phenomena (e.g., halos, rainbows), understand their formation principles through atmospheric physics and meteorology, and explore related wider topics such as front, air mass, storm and terrestrial effects. Activities combine outdoor sky observation, data analysis, and group discussion, fostering both scientific inquiry and a deeper connection with the natural environment.

# The International Lens

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5094/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| Leader | ECA |
| Leadership Team | 24424 |
| Category | ACADEMIC Social sciences |
| Room | A201 |
| Students | 1 |
| Period | Tuesday LunchTime |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

The International Lens is for students who see a major news story and think, “I know this matters—but what is actually going on?” Our aim is to make politics, economics, and global affairs easier to understand and much more enjoyable to explore. Each week, we will choose a real event, explain the background in beginner-friendly language, and then do something interactive with it. Members might run a mock election, represent different countries in a crisis, investigate a suspicious headline, compete in a geography challenge, or debate what a government should do next. No previous knowledge is required, and nobody should feel embarrassed about asking a “basic” question. Members will also take turns sharing topics they find interesting and helping one another understand difficult ideas. Last year, we built The International Lens website, which includes beginner lessons, country profiles, event explanations, and a forum. The website will remain a useful resource, but the heart of the ECA will be learning, discussing, questioning, and discovering how surprisingly connected the world is.

| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

SCIE Philosophy Club is a discussion-based ECA for students interested in examining everyday questions more carefully. Each session begins with a question, thought experiment, short reading, film clip, or real-world case, followed by student-led discussion and argument. Topics may include free will, personal identity, morality, knowledge, language, aesthetics, political philosophy, and questions raised by new technologies such as artificial intelligence. Rather than requiring previous knowledge of philosophy, the club will introduce philosophical ideas when they become useful for understanding the question at hand. Members will learn to identify assumptions, construct arguments, respond to objections, and revise their views through discussion. We hope to create an open environment in which disagreement is treated as something to investigate rather than something that has to be resolved immediately.

## 社团介绍

Puzzle Hunt is an event where teams compete to solve a series of puzzles. It originated from MIT in 1981 and has now become one of the most popular events in universities. Puzzle Hunt is a community with diversity. Everyone can find their excellent field. In SCIE Puzzle Hunter, students will delve into different types of puzzles: logic puzzles, crosswords, cryptography, steganography, detective fiction, linguistics, etc. Through this process, we will explore useful techniques that can be used in puzzle hunt: searching, frequency analysis, brute force machines, solvers, etc. We will try past puzzle hunts like MIT Mystery Hunt and Microsoft Puzzle Hunt to feel the charm of those exciting puzzle structures and designs. Furthermore, we will form teams to participate in those ongoing hunts and compete with teams from all over the world. Like those hunts held by other universities, our ultimate goal is to write our own SCIE Puzzle Hunt in this year.

# SCIE RhythmGames

| Period | Monday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

The SCIE Rhythm Games ECA targets players interested in further understanding rhythm games and making friends with similar interests. The ECA focuses on building strong connections between players and educating players on rhythm game chart production. During each session, a designated presenter will provide a short introduction lasting 15 to 30 minutes around a typical type of rhythm game, famous rhythm game music producer, or chart production. Advanced players knowledgeable about the specific topics could volunteer to be the presenter in charge of the introductions for the weeks, which helps improve the quality of content and sense of participation. Members can stay after the introduction to attend activities such as video recording or join multi-player mode with other members. Regular activities such as song producer guessing, draw and guess, and friendly competition amongst club members will also be available. This ECA plans to provide lessons and activities that alternate between different options. The session will last from 4:45 to 5:15. The Game introduction will focus on a famous rhythm game, introducing its mechanism, style of music and art, well-known songs and characters, and strengths and weaknesses. Music producers' introductions will focus on their music groups, how their style and genre changed over time, their best songs, and the famous rhythm game songs they produced. The presenter will present the topic each week with a PowerPoint presentation and short clips of the game and music. Below is a brief draft for the first 10 ECA sessions.

## 社团介绍

UZIV has been part of SCIE for 19 years, dedicated to creating positive change through meaningful community service. Our club believes that small acts of kindness can bring lasting impact, and we design activities that connect students with both local needs and wider social concerns. Over the years, we have organised beach cleanups to protect the environment, created art portrait projects to celebrate and encourage children living with challenges, and visited children’s welfare institutions to bring care and companionship. The Charity Bazaar allows us to build a deeper connection with the charity house, as all of our revenue is donated in support of their work. It reflects our belief in collective effort: when many people come together with care and energy, they can create a kinder and more supportive community for everyone. Beyond these regular initiatives, UZIV also serves as a space for creativity and inspiration. We encourage members to design new events and contribute their own ideas, making the club a dynamic platform for student-led action. Every project we take on reflects the diversity of our members’ passions while staying true to our mission of fostering compassion and responsibility.

## 社团介绍

SCIE Robotics Team is SCIE’s student-led FIRST Tech Challenge (FTC) programme, returning for its third year. Each season, members work together to design, build, program and drive a robot for a new competition game released worldwide. FTC combines the energy and teamwork of a sport with real engineering, creativity and strategy. The team will compete in official FTC qualifying tournaments, work towards advancement to championship events. No previous robotics experience is required. New members receive structured training and can explore mechanical design, CAD, electronics, software, computer vision, match strategy, media, business and community outreach. Whether you want to machine a mechanism, write autonomous code, secure a sponsor or present to judges, there is a meaningful role for you. SCIE’s team has won an FTC qualifier, captained a finalist alliance, received second place for the Inspire Award and won the Innovate Award; its 2025–26 robot also reached third in the global TeleOp rankings. Beyond competition, members showcase robotics at school events, visit technology companies, deliver STEM activities for younger students and collaborate with other FIRST teams to expand impactful outreach in China and the wider world. Come build ambitious things, learn from failure, compete and have fun with a supportive team. FIRST Impact Report: https://www.firstinspires.org/hubfs/web/about/report/annual_report_2025.pdf

## 社团介绍

The University Mathematics Society is a vibrant community for students passionate about the beauty and depth of mathematical thinking. Our activities go beyond the standard curriculum, exploring rigorous topics such as real analysis, linear algebra, number theory, and group theory. We delve into epsilon-delta proofs, eigenvector decompositions, and the subtleties of convergence—building a foundation that turns abstract definitions into powerful tools. Beyond exam preparation, we host guest lectures, problem-solving competitions, and collaborative study groups. Whether you are aiming for Cambridge, Oxford, or simply love a good mathematical challenge, our society offers a welcoming and intellectually stimulating environment. Join us to sharpen your mind, share ideas, and celebrate the elegance of mathematics together. Everyone is welcome—from first-year enthusiasts to final-year experts.

| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

This year, "University Physics" isn't just about memorizing equations—it's about fundamentally changing how we see the universe. We’ll step beyond the standard high school curriculum, moving from the familiar Newtonian world of pushing and pulling forces into the beautifully elegant realm of analytical mechanics. By adopting the Lagrangian approach, we’ll explore the principle of least action, discovering how complex systems can be understood entirely through energy rather than messy vectors. But we won't stop at classical mechanics. We’re also diving into the weird, wonderful frontiers of modern physics. Through concepts like quantum mechanics and special relativity, we’ll unpack mind-bending, counterintuitive ideas that perfectly explain the bizarre realities of our world. At its core, our club is about building genuine physical and mathematical intuition. We want to explore advanced problem-solving methods and theorems you won't find in standard textbooks. By tackling these challenges together, we'll equip you with the strong intuition and deep understanding needed to confidently pursue higher-level physics in the future.

## 社团介绍

SCIE Visual Novel Horizon is an interdisciplinary platform dedicated to exploring visual novels as a dynamic medium uniting interactive literature, visual arts, and digital technology. Guided by our motto, "Visual novels, tech, and explorations beyond the horizon," the club examines the thematic and structural depth of visual novels through multi-faceted critical frameworks, drawing insights from literature, philosophy, psychology, sociology, and ethics. In parallel with literary critique, we deconstruct the technical and creative craftsmanship required to build compelling visual novels. Members share insights into game engine programming, branching narrative structures, artistic styling, and adaptive sound design. SCIE Visual Novel Horizon provides an open, intellectually rigorous stage where students can present their analyses, share developmental techniques, and engage in candid peer debate. By embracing differing viewpoints, agreement, and constructive disagreement, the club cultivates both sharp critical thinkers and capable multimedia creators.

## 社团介绍

Our ECA is an original economic card game creation society built on a self-designed fictional continent with diverse unique nations. In previous years, our core focus was fusing formal economic academic knowledge with tabletop gameplay, as our team independently designed, balanced and refined a proprietary card game that models real-world market cycles, trade policies and resource allocation. For the upcoming academic year, we pursue dual-track progress: we continue iterating new card expansions and gameplay mechanics, while vastly expanding our lore framework—crafting core protagonists, layered character backstories, inter-nation conflicts and complete narrative arcs to deepen our immersive fantasy world. Additionally, we will launch official social media channels to consistently publish serialized worldbuilding entries, character biographies, game tutorials and behind-the-scenes development content, letting members collectively build a cohesive, self-contained original game IP.

## 社团介绍

SCIEscape is a student-led publication platform dedicated to exploring ideas in politics, philosophy, and economics beyond the boundaries of the classroom. Recognizing that many students engage deeply with complex social questions but lack a structured space to share their perspectives, We established a platform where curiosity could become public dialogue. We led a student editorial team in collaboration with history faculty members, organizing a monthly article selection process that encouraged rigorous argument, interdisciplinary thinking, and thoughtful discussion. Contributors explored topics ranging from political institutions and philosophical debates to economic issues shaping contemporary society, transforming individual interests into accessible intellectual conversations. Beyond simply publishing articles, SCIEscape created a community where students learned to refine their ideas, respond to different viewpoints, and communicate arguments to a broader audience. Through this initiative, we aimed to cultivate a culture of inquiry where students could move beyond textbook knowledge, connect academic concepts with real-world issues, and participate actively in shaping intellectual discourse within the school community.

# Zhuangzi Discussion Club

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5154/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

## 基本信息

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 174 |
| Name | Zhuangzi Discussion Club |
| Leader | BOX |
| Leadership Team |  |
| Category | ACADEMIC Others |
| Room | B424 |
| Students | 0 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

What does it mean to be free? Is success always worth pursuing? How much of our lives should be determined by grades, university offers, competition and other people's expectations? And how can we maintain a sense of balance when the world around us constantly tells us to do more, achieve more and become more? These are not merely philosophical questions. They are questions about everyday life. This ECA explores these questions through the Inner Chapters of the Zhuangzi, one of the most imaginative works of classical Chinese philosophy. Rather than studying Zhuangzi simply as an ancient Chinese thinker, students will encounter his ideas as a provocative conversation partner for the challenges of modern student life. Through stories, paradoxes, thought experiments, close reading and panel discussions, we will explore themes including freedom, perspective, comparison, success, social expectations, identity, uncertainty, change and inner balance. This ECA creates a space where students can step outside the pressure of everyday academic life, question assumptions, encounter different perspectives, and reflect on what they genuinely value. Ultimately, Zhuangzi invites us to consider a deceptively simple question: How can we live freely in a world that constantly tells us who we should be?

- Bo — Teacher bo.xie@scie.com.cn

## 社团介绍

The Science, Technology, and Society (STS) Club - "Innovating Today, Transforming Tomorrow" - serves as a dynamic platform for students to explore the intricate relationships between scientific advancements, technological innovations, and their societal impacts. Our mission is to foster critical thinking and interdisciplinary collaboration, empowering students to understand and address contemporary global challenges through the lens of STS. In addition to engaging discussions and research, the club encourages students to form teams and incubate STS-related projects that leverage technology to create positive societal change. These projects may focus on areas such as sustainable development, social equity, ethical technology, and public health. The STS Club aims to cultivate a generation of socially responsible innovators equipped to navigate and shape the future of our interconnected world. Furthermore, the club aspires to publish two issues of an STS journal annually. This initiative will provide students with the opportunity to engage in academic writing, peer review processes, and the publication of original research and essays. By contributing to this journal, members will gain practical experience in scholarly communication and deepen their understanding of the interdisciplinary nature of STS.

SCIE Scuba Diving Club is a sports-focused ECA dedicated to promoting scuba diving culture and marine environmental protection. We welcome both PADI-certified divers and ocean-loving students without certification as land volunteers. We run regular sessions on PADI OW foundational theory, diving safety and marine ecosystem knowledge. In summer, we organize certified open-water diving trips to Shenzhen Dapeng, with free equipment including BCD and wetsuits provided. Members can take part in public-welfare dives to clear marine litter, earning community service hours while contributing to ocean conservation. Our club aims to build a community for diving enthusiasts, foster marine protection awareness, and provide safe, well-organized opportunities to explore the underwater world.

## 社团介绍

Silver Impact Initiative is a student-led club dedicated to improving the lives of older adults through science, community action, and youth leadership. Each month, members explore a real-world challenge affecting older adults, learn from scientific research and experts, and develop practical ideas to address the issue. Not only do we aim to help the elderly, we also reflect upon ourselves after identifying the issues of the elderly to learn how to avoid some preventable issues. Our activities follow a four-step model: Learn → Ideate → Act → Reflect. Students may participate in research, volunteering, community projects, design, or innovation. Members also have opportunities to become Project Leaders, where they develop an idea, form a team, coordinate implementation, and measure the project’s outcomes all with the help of resources of the ECA and its members . Projects remain part of the broader Silver Impact Initiative, allowing future students to build upon previous work and creating continuity beyond individual events. Our initial topics include cancer, Alzheimer’s disease, and hearing loss, with a particular focus on developing a long-term hearing-care initiative for older adults. We aim to connect students with appropriate experts, healthcare institutions, community organizations, and nonprofit or foundation resources where possible.

Weekly sessions include teaching core concepts and problem-solving techniques, followed by discussions of challenging problems designed by club leaders. Each year, we create original problems for competitions. Purpose: We bring together students who are passionate about logical thinking and interdisciplinary problem-solving. The club provides a platform for members to exchange ideas, find like-minded peers, and support each other’s learning. Through peer teaching and collaborative discussion, we aim to strengthen SCIE students’ competitiveness and enrich campus life.

## 社团介绍

The Speedcubing Club is a dynamic and inclusive extracurricular activity dedicated to the art of solving twisty puzzles—most notably the iconic Rubik's Cube—as efficiently as possible. Whether students are complete beginners looking to solve their very first cube or experienced speedcubers aiming to shave seconds off their personal bests, this club provides a supportive and engaging environment for all skill levels. Members will learn foundational solving methods, advanced methods like CFOP (Cross, F2L, OLL, PLL), and optimal finger trick techniques. Beyond just solving puzzles, the club fosters a strong sense of community, encouraging members to share strategies, participate in friendly internal competitions, and celebrate each other's progress. Speedcubing is not just about fast hands; it challenges the mind, builds spatial awareness, and teaches perseverance. Join us to improve your dexterity, challenge your brain, and be part of a vibrant community!

## 社团介绍

Starry Musical aims to provide a platform for discussions between musical lovers and works for the publication of musicals. We welcome every musical lover, or anyone willing to know about musicals. Unlike other musical-related clubs focusing on performing musicals, we mainly create a place for non-singers and non-actors to feel the charm of musicals, which is also a promotion of the thought that “musicals are for everyone as long as you are interested in it”. We will discuss official recordings of musicals, including famous and outstanding foreign ones from Broadway or West End, and more importantly, some Chinese musicals. We want to promote and publicize that there are also good musicals in China, originally produced by Chinese playwrights and Chinese musical actors and actresses. After watching every musical, we plan to showcase our understanding and publicize the musicals by posting videos on social media. Starry Musical channel has already been registered on many platforms online. Everyone in Starry Musical would be responsible for part of the videos, including but not limited to video editing, speakers in the videos and scriptwriters. Also, we plan to link musicals to other topics, like mental illness, depending on the musicals we are watching.

The FTC Robotics ECA is a student-led team that designs, builds, and tests a robot for the FIRST Tech Challenge. Members will work together throughout the engineering process, from understanding the competition requirements and developing initial ideas to creating CAD models, manufacturing parts, assembling mechanisms, and testing the completed robot. The ECA will introduce members to practical skills such as CAD, 3D printing, mechanical assembly, tool use, and robot design. Members with different levels of experience are welcome, and more experienced students will help teach and guide newer members. Our main aim is to prepare for the FTC competition while creating a collaborative environment where members can experiment, solve problems, learn from mistakes, and turn their ideas into a working robot.

TEDxSCIE is the a student-led organisation at SCIE. Since 2017, we have run for 9 years and delivered 8 annual conferences, each bringing together many students and teachers. We turn "Ideas Worth Spreading" into practice by creating an open platform for genuine intellectual exchange. Our weekly workshops cover topic brainstorming, speech writing, verbal delivery, body language, and confidence-building. We also use internal speech competitions to help members improve through practice. The team is divided into four functional departments: Academic, Publicity, Planning, and External Relations. Members get hands-on experience in every stage of organising a large-scale event—from theme selection and speaker outreach to stage execution and on-site coordination. Last academic year, we received the Best Leadership ECA award at SCIE.

## 社团介绍

The Grove is a student-led creative community that aims to create a welcoming space where students can connect, explore new experiences and feel free to be themselves. In a busy school environment, students may sometimes feel stressed, disconnected or unsure where they belong. The Grove provides a supportive environment where students can build meaningful connections through shared experiences and collaborative activities. Rather than focusing on one fixed hobby, The Grove encourages members to explore different interests and create experiences together. Activities may include creative workshops, discussions, film sessions, hands-on projects and other ideas proposed by members. While activities can change, the experience remains the same: connecting with others, creating something together and growing as a community. Most importantly, members are not simply participants. They are encouraged to contribute ideas, take responsibility and gradually lead projects. Through this process, students develop communication, collaboration, organization and leadership skills in an authentic environment. Like a grove formed by many individual trees, The Grove grows through the unique identities and contributions of every member.

## 社团介绍

The club focuses on WotaGei, a dynamic Japanese pop dance born out of the glowsticks moves originally for fans to express the support to the people they like on stages. The dancer usually holds two high-brightness glowsticks, and perform intensely, leaving beautiful trails of light in the air. The study of OtaGei, the varient of WotaGei will also be involved in the club activities. Unlike WotaGei, OtaGei uses no glowsticks, and is typically not considered as stage performances. Instead, it is more of a relax meathod for the dancer to immerse and enjoy the background music. For the estimated 20 years since WotaGei has been founded, numerous enthusiasts have been creating new Wotagei choreography, also known as Waza. There will always be new things to learn and perfect in this unique foreign dance.

This sport is super fun; you won't have any aggressive body contact; instead, all you need to do is work closely with your teammates, act fast, and try your best strategy and angle to attack the net to get points. ## 社团介绍

The International Lens is for students who see a major news story and think, “I know this matters—but what is actually going on?” Our aim is to make politics, economics, and global affairs easier to understand and much more enjoyable to explore. Each week, we will choose a real event, explain the background in beginner-friendly language, and then do something interactive with it. Members might run a mock election, represent different countries in a crisis, investigate a suspicious headline, compete in a geography challenge, or debate what a government should do next. No previous knowledge is required, and nobody should feel embarrassed about asking a “basic” question. Members will also take turns sharing topics they find interesting and helping one another understand difficult ideas. Last year, we built The International Lens website, which includes beginner lessons, country profiles, event explanations, and a forum. The website will remain a useful resource, but the heart of the ECA will be learning, discussing, questioning, and discovering how surprisingly connected the world is.

## 社团介绍

Theoretical Politics Club is a discussion-based ECA for students interested in political theory, comparative politics, and the functioning of different political systems. In the first several sessions, members will be introduced to major political theories and concepts, such as liberalism, conservatism, socialism, realism, nationalism, democracy, authoritarianism, legitimacy, state power, and political institutions. After building this theoretical foundation, the club will move toward case-based analysis. Members will examine selected historical and contemporary political regimes, comparing how institutions, ideology, leadership, economic conditions, and social forces shape political outcomes. Sessions will mainly consist of short presentations, guided discussion, debates, and group analysis. The purpose is not to promote any particular political position, but to encourage members to understand political systems from multiple perspectives and develop evidence-based arguments.

## 社团介绍

UZIV has been part of SCIE for 19 years, dedicated to creating positive change through meaningful community service. Our club believes that small acts of kindness can bring lasting impact, and we design activities that connect students with both local needs and wider social concerns. Over the years, we have organised beach cleanups to protect the environment, created art portrait projects to celebrate and encourage children living with challenges, and visited children’s welfare institutions to bring care and companionship. The Charity Bazaar allows us to build a deeper connection with the charity house, as all of our revenue is donated in support of their work. It reflects our belief in collective effort: when many people come together with care and energy, they can create a kinder and more supportive community for everyone. Beyond these regular initiatives, UZIV also serves as a space for creativity and inspiration. We encourage members to design new events and contribute their own ideas, making the club a dynamic platform for student-led action. Every project we take on reflects the diversity of our members’ passions while staying true to our mission of fostering compassion and responsibility.

The University Mathematics Society is a vibrant community for students passionate about the beauty and depth of mathematical thinking. Our activities go beyond the standard curriculum, exploring rigorous topics such as real analysis, linear algebra, number theory, and group theory. We delve into epsilon-delta proofs, eigenvector decompositions, and the subtleties of convergence—building a foundation that turns abstract definitions into powerful tools. Beyond exam preparation, we host guest lectures, problem-solving competitions, and collaborative study groups. Whether you are aiming for Cambridge, Oxford, or simply love a good mathematical challenge, our society offers a welcoming and intellectually stimulating environment. Join us to sharpen your mind, share ideas, and celebrate the elegance of mathematics together. Everyone is welcome—from first-year enthusiasts to final-year experts.

# University Physics

| Period | Tuesday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

This year, "University Physics" isn't just about memorizing equations—it's about fundamentally changing how we see the universe. We’ll step beyond the standard high school curriculum, moving from the familiar Newtonian world of pushing and pulling forces into the beautifully elegant realm of analytical mechanics. By adopting the Lagrangian approach, we’ll explore the principle of least action, discovering how complex systems can be understood entirely through energy rather than messy vectors. But we won't stop at classical mechanics. We’re also diving into the weird, wonderful frontiers of modern physics. Through concepts like quantum mechanics and special relativity, we’ll unpack mind-bending, counterintuitive ideas that perfectly explain the bizarre realities of our world. At its core, our club is about building genuine physical and mathematical intuition. We want to explore advanced problem-solving methods and theorems you won't find in standard textbooks. By tackling these challenges together, we'll equip you with the strong intuition and deep understanding needed to confidently pursue higher-level physics in the future.

Our ECA is an original economic card game creation society built on a self-designed fictional continent with diverse unique nations. In previous years, our core focus was fusing formal economic academic knowledge with tabletop gameplay, as our team independently designed, balanced and refined a proprietary card game that models real-world market cycles, trade policies and resource allocation. For the upcoming academic year, we pursue dual-track progress: we continue iterating new card expansions and gameplay mechanics, while vastly expanding our lore framework—crafting core protagonists, layered character backstories, inter-nation conflicts and complete narrative arcs to deepen our immersive fantasy world. Additionally, we will launch official social media channels to consistently publish serialized worldbuilding entries, character biographies, game tutorials and behind-the-scenes development content, letting members collectively build a cohesive, self-contained original game IP.

# Zhuangzi Discussion Club

- 详情页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/5154/profile/
- 列表页: https://www.alevel.com.cn/user/s23433/ecaclass/all_eca/

| 字段 | 内容 |
| --- | --- |
| NO. | 224 |
| Name | Zhuangzi Discussion Club |
| Leader | BOX |
| Leadership Team |  |
| Category | ACADEMIC Others |
| Room | B424 |
| Students | 0 |
| Period | Thursday Period11 |
| Space | 0.0/50MB |

## 社团介绍

What does it mean to be free? Is success always worth pursuing? How much of our lives should be determined by grades, university offers, competition and other people's expectations? And how can we maintain a sense of balance when the world around us constantly tells us to do more, achieve more and become more? These are not merely philosophical questions. They are questions about everyday life. This ECA explores these questions through the Inner Chapters of the Zhuangzi, one of the most imaginative works of classical Chinese philosophy. Rather than studying Zhuangzi simply as an ancient Chinese thinker, students will encounter his ideas as a provocative conversation partner for the challenges of modern student life. Through stories, paradoxes, thought experiments, close reading and panel discussions, we will explore themes including freedom, perspective, comparison, success, social expectations, identity, uncertainty, change and inner balance. This ECA creates a space where students can step outside the pressure of everyday academic life, question assumptions, encounter different perspectives, and reflect on what they genuinely value. Ultimately, Zhuangzi invites us to consider a deceptively simple question: How can we live freely in a world that constantly tells us who we should be?

## 负责人

- Bo — Teacher bo.xie@scie.com.cn

## 活动信息

Date: Thursday
Time: Period 11 (First period after school)
Classroom: B424

## 社团介绍

ECA description 200w Our iHOSA Club is a student-and-teacher-led organization dedicated to exploring biology, psychology, and health wellbeing through academic challenges, community service, and leadership development. Aligned with iHOSA, we focus on three aspects: 1. Dominate iHOSA competitions. Master subjects like Biology, Behavioral Health, and Pathophysiology to conquer the ATC challenges. 2. Create Health Campaigns. Design powerful PSAs and workshops to drive real change in our community, meanwhile expanding this ECA’s worth to the greater society through social media. 3. Learn from the best. We host iHOSA veterans to share their winning strategies and inspire the next generation of health science innovators. Through both lectures and hands-on promotions, we foster scientific curiosity, teamwork, and real-world impact.

## 社团介绍

We are an ECA that is coe-led by two student members ,our final goal is to compile and publish a ecnomics newspaper that will be distributed across the SCIE campus ,thus both enriching the students after -class lives and also providing an interesting way for them to learn about economics,students that join our ECA will have the chance to work with us together to compile the newspaper .However in the mean time we will also hold other classes to the students who join our ECA on other economics related topics such as study tips on economics and review of hard topics in both G1 and G2 .As well as other useful economic facts such as stock marketing.
